Method code:
312 |   private void setSourceInfo(Node irNode, AstNode node) {
313 |     if (irNode.getLineno() == -1) {
314 |       // If we didn't already set the line, then set it now. This avoids
315 |       // cases like ParenthesizedExpression where we just return a previous
316 |       // node, but don't want the new node to get its parent's line number.
317 |       int lineno = node.getLineno();
318 |       irNode.setLineno(lineno);
319 |       int charno = position2charno(node.getAbsolutePosition());
320 |       irNode.setCharno(charno);
321 |       maybeSetLengthFrom(irNode, node);
322 |     }
323 |   }

===== M0007 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.initOptions(CompilerOptions)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 227-290
Documentation:
/**
   * Initialize the compiler options. Only necessary if you're not doing
   * a normal compile() job.
   */
Method code:
227 |   public void initOptions(CompilerOptions options) {
228 |     this.options = options;
229 |     if (errorManager == null) {
230 |       if (outStream == null) {
231 |         setErrorManager(
232 |             new LoggerErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), logger));
233 |       } else {
234 |         PrintStreamErrorManager printer =
235 |             new PrintStreamErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), outStream);
236 |         printer.setSummaryDetailLevel(options.summaryDetailLevel);
237 |         setErrorManager(printer);
238 |       }
239 |     }
240 | 
241 |     // DiagnosticGroups override the plain checkTypes option.
242 |     if (options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
243 |       options.checkTypes = true;
244 |     } else if (options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
245 |       options.checkTypes = false;
246 |     } else if (!options.checkTypes) {
247 |       // If DiagnosticGroups did not override the plain checkTypes
248 |       // option, and checkTypes is enabled, then turn off the
249 |       // parser type warnings.
250 |       options.setWarningLevel(
251 |           DiagnosticGroup.forType(
252 |               RhinoErrorReporter.TYPE_PARSE_ERROR),
253 |           CheckLevel.OFF);
254 |     }
255 | 
256 |     if (options.checkGlobalThisLevel.isOn() &&
257 |         !options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS)) {
258 |       options.setWarningLevel(
259 |           DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS,
260 |           options.checkGlobalThisLevel);
261 |     }
262 | 
263 |     if (options.getLanguageIn() == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT5_STRICT) {
264 |       options.setWarningLevel(
265 |           DiagnosticGroups.ES5_STRICT,
266 |           CheckLevel.ERROR);
267 |     }
268 | 
269 |     // Initialize the warnings guard.
270 |     List<WarningsGuard> guards = Lists.newArrayList();
271 |     guards.add(
272 |         new SuppressDocWarningsGuard(
273 |             getDiagnosticGroups().getRegisteredGroups()));
274 |     guards.add(options.getWarningsGuard());
275 | 
276 |     ComposeWarningsGuard composedGuards = new ComposeWarningsGuard(guards);
277 | 
278 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
279 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
280 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
281 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
282 |     // error off.
283 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
284 |         !composedGuards.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES)) {
285 |       composedGuards.addGuard(new DiagnosticGroupWarningsGuard(
286 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF));
287 |     }
288 | 
289 |     this.warningsGuard = composedGuards;
290 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.IRFactory(String,StaticSourceFile,Config,ErrorReporter)

// TODO(johnlenz): Consider creating a template pool for ORIGINALNAME_PROP.
Method code:
123 |   private IRFactory(String sourceString,
124 |                     StaticSourceFile sourceFile,
125 |                     Config config,
126 |                     ErrorReporter errorReporter) {
127 |     this.sourceString = sourceString;
128 |     this.sourceFile = sourceFile;
129 | 
130 |     // Sometimes this will be null in tests.
131 |     this.sourceName = sourceFile == null ? null : sourceFile.getName();
132 |

134 |     this.errorReporter = errorReporter;
135 |     this.transformDispatcher = new TransformDispatcher();
136 |     // The template node properties are applied to all nodes in this transform.
137 |     this.templateNode = createTemplateNode();
138 | 
139 |     switch (config.languageMode) {
140 |       case ECMASCRIPT3:

144 |       case ECMASCRIPT5:

146 |         break;
147 |       case ECMASCRIPT5_STRICT:
148 |         reservedKeywords = ES5_STRICT_RESERVED_KEYWORDS;
149 |         break;
150 |       default:
151 |         throw new IllegalStateException("unknown language mode");
152 |     }
153 |   }

   * Verifies that at least one module has been provided and that the first one

393 |   private void checkFirstModule(List<JSModule> modules) {

===== M0023 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.AstValidator.validateStatement(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/AstValidator.java
Original range: 93-153
Documentation:

Method code:
93 |   public void validateStatement(Node n) {
94 |     switch (n.getType()) {
95 |       case Token.LABEL:
96 |         validateLabel(n);
97 |         return;
98 |       case Token.BLOCK:
99 |         validateBlock(n);
100 |         return;
101 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
102 |         validateFunctionStatement(n);
103 |         return;
104 |       case Token.WITH:
105 |         validateWith(n);
106 |         return;
107 |       case Token.FOR:
108 |         validateFor(n);
109 |         return;
110 |       case Token.WHILE:
111 |         validateWhile(n);
112 |         return;
113 |       case Token.DO:
114 |         validateDo(n);
115 |         return;
116 |       case Token.SWITCH:
117 |         validateSwitch(n);
118 |         return;
119 |       case Token.IF:
120 |         validateIf(n);
121 |         return;
122 |       case Token.VAR:
123 |         validateVar(n);
124 |         return;
125 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
126 |         validateExprStmt(n);
127 |         return;
128 |       case Token.RETURN:
129 |         validateReturn(n);
130 |         return;
131 |       case Token.THROW:
132 |         validateThrow(n);
133 |         return;
134 |       case Token.TRY:
135 |         validateTry(n);
136 |         return;
137 |       case Token.BREAK:
138 |         validateBreak(n);
139 |         return;
140 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
141 |         validateContinue(n);
142 |         return;
143 |       case Token.EMPTY:
144 |         validateChildless(n);
145 |         return;
146 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
147 |         validateChildless(n);
148 |         return;
149 |       default:
150 |         violation("Expected statement but was "
151 |             + Token.name(n.getType()) + ".", n);
152 |     }
153 |   }

===== M0025 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.AstValidator.validateMinimumChildCount(Node,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/AstValidator.java
Original range: 790-805
Documentation:

Method code:
790 |   private void validateMinimumChildCount(Node n, int i) {
791 |     boolean valid = false;
792 |     if (i == 1) {
793 |       valid = n.hasChildren();
794 |     } else if (i == 2) {
795 |       valid = n.hasMoreThanOneChild();
796 |     } else {
797 |       valid = n.getChildCount() >= i;
798 |     }
799 | 
800 |     if (!valid) {
801 |       violation(
802 |           "Expected at least " + i + " children, but was "
803 |               + n.getChildCount(), n);
804 |     }
805 |   }

116 |   private static final String propToString(int propType) {
117 |       switch (propType) {
118 |         case BRACELESS_TYPE:     return "braceless_type";
119 |         case VAR_ARGS_NAME:      return "var_args_name";
120 |         case SOURCENAME_PROP:    return "sourcename";

/**
   * This function adds a comma-separated list as is specified by an ARRAYLIT
   * node with the associated skipIndexes array.  This is a space optimization
   * since we avoid creating a whole Node object for each empty array literal
   * slot.
   * @param firstInList The first in the node list (chained through the next
   * property).
   */
Method code:
912 |   void addArrayList(Node firstInList) {
913 |     boolean lastWasEmpty = false;
914 |     for (Node n = firstInList; n != null; n = n.getNext()) {
915 |       if (n != firstInList) {
916 |         cc.listSeparator();
917 |       }
918 |       addExpr(n, 1);
919 |       lastWasEmpty = n.isEmpty();
920 |     }
921 | 
922 |     if (lastWasEmpty) {
923 |       cc.listSeparator();
924 |     }
925 |   }

===== M0064 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.RemoveUnusedVars.removeUnreferencedFunctionArgs(Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/RemoveUnusedVars.java
Original range: 360-389
Documentation:
/**
   * Removes unreferenced arguments from a function declaration and when
   * possible the function's callSites.
   *
   * @param fnScope The scope inside the function
   */
Method code:
360 |   private void removeUnreferencedFunctionArgs(Scope fnScope) {
361 |     // TODO(johnlenz): Update type registry for function signature changes.
362 | 
363 |     Node function = fnScope.getRootNode();
364 | 
365 |     Preconditions.checkState(function.isFunction());
366 |     if (NodeUtil.isGetOrSetKey(function.getParent())) {
367 |       // The parameters object literal setters can not be removed.
368 |       return;
369 |     }
370 | 
371 |     Node argList = getFunctionArgList(function);
372 |     boolean modifyCallers = modifyCallSites
373 |         && callSiteOptimizer.canModifyCallers(function);
374 |     if (!modifyCallers) {
375 |       // Strip unreferenced args off the end of the function declaration.
376 |       Node lastArg;
377 |       while ((lastArg = argList.getLastChild()) != null) {
378 |         Var var = fnScope.getVar(lastArg.getString());
379 |         if (!referenced.contains(var)) {
380 |           argList.removeChild(lastArg);
381 |           compiler.reportCodeChange();
382 |         } else {
383 |           break;
384 |         }
385 |       }
386 |     } else {
387 |       callSiteOptimizer.optimize(fnScope, referenced);
388 |     }
389 |   }

===== M0065 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.transformAsString(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 398-411
Documentation:
/**
     * Transforms the given node and then sets its type to Token.STRING if it
     * was Token.NAME. If its type was already Token.STRING, then quotes it.
     * Used for properties, as the old AST uses String tokens, while the new one
     * uses Name tokens for unquoted strings. For example, in
     * var o = {'a' : 1, b: 2};
     * the string 'a' is quoted, while the name b is turned into a string, but
     * unquoted.
     */
Method code:
398 |     private Node transformAsString(AstNode n) {
399 |       Node ret;
400 |       if (n instanceof Name) {
401 |         ret = transformNameAsString((Name)n);
402 |       } else if (n instanceof NumberLiteral) {
403 |         ret = transformNumberAsString((NumberLiteral)n);
404 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
405 |       } else {
406 |         ret = transform(n);
407 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
408 |       }
409 |       Preconditions.checkState(ret.isString());
410 |       return ret;
411 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.removeProp(PropListItem,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 773-786
Documentation:
/**
   * @param item The item to inspect
   * @param propType The property to look for
   * @return The replacement list if the property was removed, or
   *   'item' otherwise.
   */
Method code:
773 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {
774 |     if (item == null) {
775 |       return null;
776 |     } else if (item.getType() == propType) {
777 |       return item.getNext();
778 |     } else {
779 |       PropListItem result = removeProp(item.getNext(), propType);
780 |       if (result != item.getNext()) {
781 |         return item.chain(result);
782 |       } else {
783 |         return item;
784 |       }
785 |     }
786 |   }

===== M0078 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 459-474
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * Ecma-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
459 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
460 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
461 |       Set<String> directives = null;
462 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
463 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
464 |         if (directives == null) {
465 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
466 |         } else {
467 |           directives.add(directive);
468 |         }
469 |       }
470 | 
471 |       if (directives != null) {
472 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
473 |       }
474 |     }

===== M0085 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.isEquivalentTo(Node,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1482-1529
Documentation:
/**
   * @param compareJsType Whether to compare the JSTypes of the nodes.
   * @param recurse Whether to compare the children of the current node, if
   *    not only the the count of the children are compared.
   * @return Whether this node is equivalent semantically to the provided node.
   */
Method code:
1482 |   boolean isEquivalentTo(Node node, boolean compareJsType, boolean recurse) {
1483 |     if (type != node.getType()
1484 |         || getChildCount() != node.getChildCount()
1485 |         || this.getClass() != node.getClass()) {
1486 |       return false;
1487 |     }
1488 | 
1489 |     if (compareJsType && !JSType.isEquivalent(jsType, node.getJSType())) {
1490 |       return false;
1491 |     }
1492 | 
1493 |     if (type == Token.INC || type == Token.DEC) {
1494 |       int post1 = this.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1495 |       int post2 = node.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1496 |       if (post1 != post2) {
1497 |         return false;
1498 |       }
1499 |     } else if (type == Token.STRING) {
1500 |       int quoted1 = this.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1501 |       int quoted2 = node.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1502 |       if (quoted1 != quoted2) {
1503 |         return false;
1504 |       }
1505 | 
1506 |       int slashV1 = this.getIntProp(SLASH_V);
1507 |       int slashV2 = node.getIntProp(SLASH_V);
1508 |       if (slashV1 != slashV2) {
1509 |         return false;
1510 |       }
1511 |     } else if (type == Token.CALL) {
1512 |       if (this.getBooleanProp(FREE_CALL) != node.getBooleanProp(FREE_CALL)) {
1513 |         return false;
1514 |       }
1515 |     }
1516 | 
1517 |     if (recurse) {
1518 |       Node n, n2;
1519 |       for (n = first, n2 = node.first;
1520 |            n != null;
1521 |            n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1522 |         if (!n.isEquivalentTo(n2, compareJsType, true)) {
1523 |           return false;
1524 |         }
1525 |       }
1526 |     }
1527 | 
1528 |     return true;
1529 |   }

===== M0098 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.checkForStateChangeHelper(Node,boolean,AbstractCompiler)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 784-927
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if some node in n's subtree changes application state.
   * If {@code checkForNewObjects} is true, we assume that newly created
   * mutable objects (like object literals) change state. Otherwise, we assume
   * that they have no side effects.
   */
Method code:
784 |   private static boolean checkForStateChangeHelper(
785 |       Node n, boolean checkForNewObjects, AbstractCompiler compiler) {
786 |     // Rather than id which ops may have side effects, id the ones
787 |     // that we know to be safe
788 |     switch (n.getType()) {
789 |       // other side-effect free statements and expressions
790 |       case Token.AND:
791 |       case Token.BLOCK:
792 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
793 |       case Token.HOOK:
794 |       case Token.IF:
795 |       case Token.IN:
796 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST:
797 |       case Token.NUMBER:
798 |       case Token.OR:
799 |       case Token.THIS:
800 |       case Token.TRUE:
801 |       case Token.FALSE:
802 |       case Token.NULL:
803 |       case Token.STRING:
804 |       case Token.SWITCH:
805 |       case Token.TRY:
806 |       case Token.EMPTY:
807 |         break;
808 | 
809 |       // Throws are by definition side effects
810 |       case Token.THROW:
811 |         return true;
812 | 
813 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
814 |         if (checkForNewObjects) {
815 |           return true;
816 |         }
817 |         for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
818 |           if (checkForStateChangeHelper(
819 |                   c.getFirstChild(), checkForNewObjects, compiler)) {
820 |             return true;
821 |           }
822 |         }
823 |         return false;
824 | 
825 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
826 |       case Token.REGEXP:
827 |         if (checkForNewObjects) {
828 |           return true;
829 |         }
830 |         break;
831 | 
832 |       case Token.VAR:    // empty var statement (no declaration)
833 |       case Token.NAME:   // variable by itself
834 |         if (n.getFirstChild() != null) {
835 |           return true;
836 |         }
837 |         break;
838 | 
839 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
840 |         // Function expressions don't have side-effects, but function
841 |         // declarations change the namespace. Either way, we don't need to
842 |         // check the children, since they aren't executed at declaration time.
843 |         return checkForNewObjects || !isFunctionExpression(n);
844 | 
845 |       case Token.NEW:
846 |         if (checkForNewObjects) {
847 |           return true;
848 |         }
849 | 
850 |         if (!constructorCallHasSideEffects(n)) {
851 |           // loop below will see if the constructor parameters have
852 |           // side-effects
853 |           break;
854 |         }
855 |         return true;
856 | 
857 |       case Token.CALL:
858 |         // calls to functions that have no side effects have the no
859 |         // side effect property set.
860 |         if (!functionCallHasSideEffects(n, compiler)) {
861 |           // loop below will see if the function parameters have
862 |           // side-effects
863 |           break;
864 |         }
865 |         return true;
866 | 
867 |       default:
868 |         if (isSimpleOperatorType(n.getType())) {
869 |           break;
870 |         }
871 | 
872 |         if (isAssignmentOp(n)) {
873 |           Node assignTarget = n.getFirstChild();
874 |           if (assignTarget.isName()) {
875 |             return true;
876 |           }
877 | 
878 |           // Assignments will have side effects if
879 |           // a) The RHS has side effects, or
880 |           // b) The LHS has side effects, or
881 |           // c) A name on the LHS will exist beyond the life of this statement.
882 |           if (checkForStateChangeHelper(
883 |                   n.getFirstChild(), checkForNewObjects, compiler) ||
884 |               checkForStateChangeHelper(
885 |                   n.getLastChild(), checkForNewObjects, compiler)) {
886 |             return true;
887 |           }
888 | 
889 |           if (isGet(assignTarget)) {
890 |             // If the object being assigned to is a local object, don't
891 |             // consider this a side-effect as it can't be referenced
892 |             // elsewhere.  Don't do this recursively as the property might
893 |             // be an alias of another object, unlike a literal below.
894 |             Node current = assignTarget.getFirstChild();
895 |             if (evaluatesToLocalValue(current)) {
896 |               return false;
897 |             }
898 | 
899 |             // A literal value as defined by "isLiteralValue" is guaranteed
900 |             // not to be an alias, or any components which are aliases of
901 |             // other objects.
902 |             // If the root object is a literal don't consider this a
903 |             // side-effect.
904 |             while (isGet(current)) {
905 |               current = current.getFirstChild();
906 |             }
907 | 
908 |             return !isLiteralValue(current, true);
909 |           } else {
910 |             // TODO(johnlenz): remove this code and make this an exception. This
911 |             // is here only for legacy reasons, the AST is not valid but
912 |             // preserve existing behavior.
913 |             return !isLiteralValue(assignTarget, true);
914 |           }
915 |         }
916 | 
917 |         return true;
918 |     }
919 | 
920 |     for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
921 |       if (checkForStateChangeHelper(c, checkForNewObjects, compiler)) {
922 |         return true;
923 |       }
924 |     }
925 | 
926 |     return false;
927 |   }

857 |   private int[] getSortedPropTypes() {

859 |     for (PropListItem x = propListHead; x != null; x = x.getNext()) {
860 |       count++;
861 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseRoots(List<Node>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 288-311
Documentation:

Method code:
288 |   public void traverseRoots(List<Node> roots) {
289 |     if (roots.isEmpty()) {
290 |       return;
291 |     }
292 | 
293 |     try {
294 |       Node scopeRoot = roots.get(0).getParent();
295 |       Preconditions.checkState(scopeRoot != null);
296 | 
297 |       inputId = NodeUtil.getInputId(scopeRoot);
298 |       sourceName = "";
299 |       curNode = scopeRoot;
300 |       pushScope(scopeRoot);
301 | 
302 |       for (Node root : roots) {
303 |         Preconditions.checkState(root.getParent() == scopeRoot);
304 |         traverseBranch(root, scopeRoot);
305 |       }
306 | 
307 |       popScope();
308 |     } catch (Exception unexpectedException) {
309 |       throwUnexpectedException(unexpectedException);
310 |     }
311 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PrepareAst.PrepareAnnotations.annotateCalls(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PrepareAst.java
Original range: 164-180
Documentation:
/**
     * There are two types of calls we are interested in calls without explicit
     * "this" values (what we are call "free" calls) and direct call to eval.
     */
Method code:
164 |     private void annotateCalls(Node n) {
165 |       Preconditions.checkState(n.isCall());
166 | 
167 |       // Keep track of of the "this" context of a call.  A call without an
168 |       // explicit "this" is a free call.
169 |       Node first = n.getFirstChild();
170 |       if (!NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
171 |         n.putBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL, true);
172 |       }
173 | 
174 |       // Keep track of the context in which eval is called. It is important
175 |       // to distinguish between "(0, eval)()" and "eval()".
176 |       if (first.isName() &&
177 |           "eval".equals(first.getString())) {
178 |         first.putBooleanProp(Node.DIRECT_EVAL, true);
179 |       }
180 |     }

===== M0118 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addNumber(double)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 233-259
Documentation:

Method code:
233 |   void addNumber(double x) {
234 |     // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of x- -4 as
235 |     // x--4 (which is a syntax error).
236 |     char prev = getLastChar();
237 |     if (x < 0 && prev == '-') {
238 |       add(" ");
239 |     }
240 | 
241 |     if ((long) x == x && !isNegativeZero(x)) {
242 |       long value = (long) x;
243 |       long mantissa = value;
244 |       int exp = 0;
245 |       if (Math.abs(x) >= 100) {
246 |         while (mantissa / 10 * Math.pow(10, exp + 1) == value) {
247 |           mantissa /= 10;
248 |           exp++;
249 |         }
250 |       }
251 |       if (exp > 2) {
252 |         add(Long.toString(mantissa) + "E" + Integer.toString(exp));
253 |       } else {
254 |         add(Long.toString(value));
255 |       }
256 |     } else {
257 |       add(String.valueOf(x));
258 |     }
259 |   }

===== M0119 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.RemoveUnusedVars.Assign.maybeCreateAssign(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/RemoveUnusedVars.java
Original range: 915-941
Documentation:
/**
     * If this is an assign to a variable or its property, return it.
     * Otherwise, return null.
     */
Method code:
915 |     static Assign maybeCreateAssign(Node assignNode) {
916 |       Preconditions.checkState(NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(assignNode));
917 | 
918 |       // Skip one level of GETPROPs or GETELEMs.
919 |       //
920 |       // Don't skip more than one level, because then we get into
921 |       // situations where assigns to properties of properties will always
922 |       // trigger side-effects, and the variable they're on cannot be removed.
923 |       boolean isPropAssign = false;
924 |       Node current = assignNode.getFirstChild();
925 |       if (NodeUtil.isGet(current)) {
926 |         current = current.getFirstChild();
927 |         isPropAssign = true;
928 | 
929 |         if (current.isGetProp() &&
930 |             current.getLastChild().getString().equals("prototype")) {
931 |           // Prototype properties sets should be considered like normal
932 |           // property sets.
933 |           current = current.getFirstChild();
934 |         }
935 |       }
936 | 
937 |       if (current.isName()) {
938 |         return new Assign(assignNode, current, isPropAssign);
939 |       }
940 |       return null;
941 |     }

===== M0122 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.RemoveUnusedVars.removeUnreferencedVars()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/RemoveUnusedVars.java
Original range: 780-846
Documentation:
/**
   * Removes any vars in the scope that were not referenced. Removes any
   * assignments to those variables as well.
   */
Method code:
780 |   private void removeUnreferencedVars() {
781 |     CodingConvention convention = codingConvention;
782 | 
783 |     for (Iterator<Var> it = maybeUnreferenced.iterator(); it.hasNext(); ) {
784 |       Var var = it.next();
785 | 
786 |       // Remove calls to inheritance-defining functions where the unreferenced
787 |       // class is the subclass.
788 |       for (Node exprCallNode : inheritsCalls.get(var)) {
789 |         NodeUtil.removeChild(exprCallNode.getParent(), exprCallNode);
790 |         compiler.reportCodeChange();
791 |       }
792 | 
793 |       // Regardless of what happens to the original declaration,
794 |       // we need to remove all assigns, because they may contain references
795 |       // to other unreferenced variables.
796 |       removeAllAssigns(var);
797 | 
798 |       compiler.addToDebugLog("Unreferenced var: " + var.name);
799 |       Node nameNode = var.nameNode;
800 |       Node toRemove = nameNode.getParent();
801 |       Node parent = toRemove.getParent();
802 | 
803 |       Preconditions.checkState(
804 |           toRemove.isVar() ||
805 |           toRemove.isFunction() ||
806 |           toRemove.isParamList() &&
807 |           parent.isFunction(),
808 |           "We should only declare vars and functions and function args");
809 | 
810 |       if (toRemove.isParamList() &&
811 |           parent.isFunction()) {
812 |         // Don't remove function arguments here. That's a special case
813 |         // that's taken care of in removeUnreferencedFunctionArgs.
814 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(toRemove)) {
815 |         if (!preserveFunctionExpressionNames) {
816 |           toRemove.getFirstChild().setString("");
817 |           compiler.reportCodeChange();
818 |         }
819 |         // Don't remove bleeding functions.
820 |       } else if (parent != null &&
821 |           parent.isFor() &&
822 |           parent.getChildCount() < 4) {
823 |         // foreach iterations have 3 children. Leave them alone.
824 |       } else if (toRemove.isVar() &&
825 |           nameNode.hasChildren() &&
826 |           NodeUtil.mayHaveSideEffects(nameNode.getFirstChild())) {
827 |         // If this is a single var declaration, we can at least remove the
828 |         // declaration itself and just leave the value, e.g.,
829 |         // var a = foo(); => foo();
830 |         if (toRemove.getChildCount() == 1) {
831 |           parent.replaceChild(toRemove,
832 |               IR.exprResult(nameNode.removeFirstChild()));
833 |           compiler.reportCodeChange();
834 |         }
835 |       } else if (toRemove.isVar() &&
836 |           toRemove.getChildCount() > 1) {
837 |         // For var declarations with multiple names (i.e. var a, b, c),
838 |         // only remove the unreferenced name
839 |         toRemove.removeChild(nameNode);
840 |         compiler.reportCodeChange();
841 |       } else if (parent != null) {
842 |         NodeUtil.removeChild(parent, toRemove);
843 |         compiler.reportCodeChange();
844 |       }
845 |     }
846 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.findDeclaredNames(Node,Node,Renamer)

Original range: 212-229
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses the current scope and collects declared names.  Does not
   * decent into functions or add CATCH exceptions.
   */
Method code:
212 |   private void findDeclaredNames(Node n, Node parent, Renamer renamer) {
213 |     // Do a shallow traversal, so don't traverse into function declarations,
214 |     // except for the name of the function itself.
215 |     if (parent == null
216 |         || !parent.isFunction()
217 |         || n == parent.getFirstChild()) {
218 |       if (NodeUtil.isVarDeclaration(n)) {
219 |         renamer.addDeclaredName(n.getString());
220 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(n)) {
221 |         Node nameNode = n.getFirstChild();
222 |         renamer.addDeclaredName(nameNode.getString());
223 |       }
224 | 
225 |       for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
226 |         findDeclaredNames(c, n, renamer);
227 |       }
228 |     }
229 |   }

/**
   * Reduce the alternates into a non-union type.
   * If the alternates can't be accurately represented with a non-union
   * type, return null.
   */
Method code:
219 |   private JSType reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion() {
220 |     if (isAllType) {
221 |       return registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
222 |     } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
223 |       if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
224 |         return registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
225 |       } else {
226 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
227 |       }
228 |     } else {
229 |       int size = alternates.size();
230 |       if (size > maxUnionSize) {
231 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
232 |       } else if (size > 1) {
233 |         return null;
234 |       } else if (size == 1) {
235 |         return alternates.iterator().next();
236 |       } else {
237 |         return registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
238 |       }
239 |     }
240 |   }

===== M0135 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.extractForInitializer(Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 495-542
Documentation:
/**
     * Bring the initializers out of FOR loops.  These need to be placed
     * before any associated LABEL nodes. This needs to be done from the top
     * level label first so this is called as a pre-order callback (from
     * shouldTraverse).
     *
     * @param n The node to inspect.
     * @param before The node to insert the initializer before.
     * @param beforeParent The parent of the node before which the initializer
     *     will be inserted.
     */
Method code:
495 |     private void extractForInitializer(
496 |         Node n, Node before, Node beforeParent) {
497 | 
498 |       for (Node next, c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = next) {
499 |         next = c.getNext();
500 |         Node insertBefore = (before == null) ? c : before;
501 |         Node insertBeforeParent = (before == null) ? n : beforeParent;
502 |         switch (c.getType()) {
503 |           case Token.LABEL:
504 |             extractForInitializer(c, insertBefore, insertBeforeParent);
505 |             break;
506 |           case Token.FOR:
507 |             if (NodeUtil.isForIn(c)) {
508 |               Node first = c.getFirstChild();
509 |               if (first.isVar()) {
510 |                 // Transform:
511 |                 //    for (var a = 1 in b) {}
512 |                 // to:
513 |                 //    var a = 1; for (a in b) {};
514 |                 Node newStatement = first;
515 |                 // Clone just the node, to remove any initialization.
516 |                 Node name = newStatement.getFirstChild().cloneNode();
517 |                 first.getParent().replaceChild(first, name);
518 |                 insertBeforeParent.addChildBefore(newStatement, insertBefore);
519 |                 reportCodeChange("FOR-IN var declaration");
520 |               }
521 |             } else if (!c.getFirstChild().isEmpty()) {
522 |               Node init = c.getFirstChild();
523 |               Node empty = IR.empty();
524 |               empty.copyInformationFrom(c);
525 |               c.replaceChild(init, empty);
526 | 
527 |               Node newStatement;
528 |               // Only VAR statements, and expressions are allowed,
529 |               // but are handled differently.
530 |               if (init.isVar()) {
531 |                 newStatement = init;
532 |               } else {
533 |                 newStatement = NodeUtil.newExpr(init);
534 |               }
535 | 
536 |               insertBeforeParent.addChildBefore(newStatement, insertBefore);
537 |               reportCodeChange("FOR initializer");
538 |             }
539 |             break;
540 |         }
541 |       }
542 |     }

172 |     @Override
173 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
174 |       // Note: Constant properties annotations are not propagated.
175 |       if (n.isName()) {
176 |         if (n.getString().isEmpty()) {
177 |           return;
178 |         }
179 | 
180 |         JSDocInfo info = null;
181 |         // Find the JSDocInfo for a top level variable.
182 |         Var var = t.getScope().getVar(n.getString());

191 |         boolean isMarkedConstant = n.getBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
192 |         if (shouldBeConstant && !isMarkedConstant) {

434 |     void maybeLineBreak() {
435 |       if (lineBreak) {
436 |         if (sawFunction) {
437 |           startNewLine();
438 |           sawFunction = false;
439 |         }
440 |       }
441 |

443 |       // preferred break position?  We prefer to break after a

/**
   * Returns true if a name node represents a constant variable.
   *
   * <p>Determining whether a variable is constant has three steps:
   * <ol>
   * <li>In CodingConventionAnnotator, any name that matches the
   *     {@link CodingConvention#isConstant(String)} is annotated with an
   *     IS_CONSTANT_NAME property.
   * <li>The normalize pass renames any variable with the IS_CONSTANT_NAME
   *     annotation and that is initialized to a constant value with
   *     a variable name inlucding $$constant.
   * <li>Return true here if the variable includes $$constant in its name.
   * </ol>
   *
   * @param node A NAME or STRING node
   * @return True if the variable is constant

Method code:

2796 |     return node.getBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
2797 |   }

===== M0160 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLiteralValue(Node,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 585-622
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if this is a literal value. We define a literal value
   * as any node that evaluates to the same thing regardless of when or
   * where it is evaluated. So /xyz/ and [3, 5] are literals, but
   * the name a is not.
   *
   * Function literals do not meet this definition, because they
   * lexically capture variables. For example, if you have
   * <code>
   * function() { return a; }
   * </code>
   * If it is evaluated in a different scope, then it
   * captures a different variable. Even if the function did not read
   * any captured vairables directly, it would still fail this definition,
   * because it affects the lifecycle of variables in the enclosing scope.
   *
   * However, a function literal with respect to a particular scope is
   * a literal.
   *
   * @param includeFunctions If true, all function expressions will be
   *     treated as literals.
   */
Method code:
585 |   static boolean isLiteralValue(Node n, boolean includeFunctions) {
586 |     switch (n.getType()) {
587 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
588 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null;
589 |              child = child.getNext()) {
590 |           if ((!child.isEmpty()) && !isLiteralValue(child, includeFunctions)) {
591 |             return false;
592 |           }
593 |         }
594 |         return true;
595 | 
596 |       case Token.REGEXP:
597 |         // Return true only if all children are const.
598 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null;
599 |              child = child.getNext()) {
600 |           if (!isLiteralValue(child, includeFunctions)) {
601 |             return false;
602 |           }
603 |         }
604 |         return true;
605 | 
606 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
607 |         // Return true only if all values are const.
608 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null;
609 |              child = child.getNext()) {
610 |           if (!isLiteralValue(child.getFirstChild(), includeFunctions)) {
611 |             return false;
612 |           }
613 |         }
614 |         return true;
615 | 
616 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
617 |         return includeFunctions && !NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(n);
618 | 
619 |       default:
620 |         return isImmutableValue(n);
621 |     }
622 |   }

===== M0166 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.java

Documentation:

Method code:
158 |   @Override
159 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
160 |     switch (n.getType()) {
161 |       case Token.NAME:
162 |         String newName = getReplacementName(n.getString());
163 |         if (newName != null) {

185 |         break;

191 |     }

   * Traverses a node recursively. Call this once per pass.

168 |   private void traverseAndRemoveUnusedReferences(Node root) {
169 |     Scope scope = new SyntacticScopeCreator(compiler).createScope(root, null);
170 |     traverseNode(root, null, scope);
171 | 
172 |     if (removeGlobals) {
173 |       collectMaybeUnreferencedVars(scope);
174 |     }
175 | 
176 |     interpretAssigns();
177 |     removeUnreferencedVars();
178 |     for (Scope fnScope : allFunctionScopes) {
179 |       removeUnreferencedFunctionArgs(fnScope);
180 |     }
181 |   }

===== M0169 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.RemoveUnusedVars.Assign.remove()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/RemoveUnusedVars.java
Original range: 946-972
Documentation:
/**
     * Replace the current assign with its right hand side.
     */
Method code:
946 |     void remove() {
947 |       Node parent = assignNode.getParent();
948 |       if (mayHaveSecondarySideEffects) {
949 |         Node replacement = assignNode.getLastChild().detachFromParent();
950 | 
951 |         // Aggregate any expressions in GETELEMs.
952 |         for (Node current = assignNode.getFirstChild();
953 |              !current.isName();
954 |              current = current.getFirstChild()) {
955 |           if (current.isGetElem()) {
956 |             replacement = IR.comma(
957 |                 current.getLastChild().detachFromParent(), replacement);
958 |             replacement.copyInformationFrom(current);
959 |           }
960 |         }
961 | 
962 |         parent.replaceChild(assignNode, replacement);
963 |       } else {
964 |         Node gramps = parent.getParent();
965 |         if (parent.isExprResult()) {
966 |           gramps.removeChild(parent);
967 |         } else {
968 |           parent.replaceChild(assignNode,
969 |               assignNode.getLastChild().detachFromParent());
970 |         }
971 |       }
972 |     }

===== M0172 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1169-1177
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1169 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1170 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1171 |       return -1;
1172 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1173 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1174 |     } else {
1175 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1176 |     }
1177 |   }

/**
   * A "simple" operator is one whose children are expressions,
   * has no direct side-effects (unlike '+='), and has no

===== M0186 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.add(Node,Context)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 85-728
Documentation:

Method code:
85 |   void add(Node n, Context context) {
86 |     if (!cc.continueProcessing()) {
87 |       return;
88 |     }
89 | 
90 |     int type = n.getType();
91 |     String opstr = NodeUtil.opToStr(type);
92 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
93 |     Node first = n.getFirstChild();
94 |     Node last = n.getLastChild();
95 | 
96 |     // Handle all binary operators
97 |     if (opstr != null && first != last) {
98 |       Preconditions.checkState(
99 |           childCount == 2,
100 |           "Bad binary operator \"%s\": expected 2 arguments but got %s",
101 |           opstr, childCount);
102 |       int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
103 |       addLeftExpr(first, p, context);
104 |       cc.addOp(opstr, true);
105 | 
106 |       // For right-hand-side of operations, only pass context if it's
107 |       // the IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE one.
108 |       Context rhsContext = getContextForNoInOperator(context);
109 | 
110 |       // Handle associativity.
111 |       // e.g. if the parse tree is a * (b * c),
112 |       // we can simply generate a * b * c.
113 |       if (last.getType() == type &&
114 |           NodeUtil.isAssociative(type)) {
115 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
116 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(n) && NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(last)) {
117 |         // Assignments are the only right-associative binary operators
118 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
119 |       } else {
120 |         addExpr(last, p + 1, rhsContext);
121 |       }
122 |       return;
123 |     }
124 | 
125 |     cc.startSourceMapping(n);
126 | 
127 |     switch (type) {
128 |       case Token.TRY: {
129 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getNext().isBlock() &&
130 |                 !first.getNext().hasMoreThanOneChild());
131 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount >= 2 && childCount <= 3);
132 | 
133 |         add("try");
134 |         add(first, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
135 | 
136 |         // second child contains the catch block, or nothing if there
137 |         // isn't a catch block
138 |         Node catchblock = first.getNext().getFirstChild();
139 |         if (catchblock != null) {
140 |           add(catchblock);
141 |         }
142 | 
143 |         if (childCount == 3) {
144 |           add("finally");
145 |           add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
146 |         }
147 |         break;
148 |       }
149 | 
150 |       case Token.CATCH:
151 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
152 |         add("catch(");
153 |         add(first);
154 |         add(")");
155 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
156 |         break;
157 | 
158 |       case Token.THROW:
159 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
160 |         add("throw");
161 |         add(first);
162 | 
163 |         // Must have a ';' after a throw statement, otherwise safari can't
164 |         // parse this.
165 |         cc.endStatement(true);
166 |         break;
167 | 
168 |       case Token.RETURN:
169 |         add("return");
170 |         if (childCount == 1) {
171 |           add(first);
172 |         } else {
173 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
174 |         }
175 |         cc.endStatement();
176 |         break;
177 | 
178 |       case Token.VAR:
179 |         if (first != null) {
180 |           add("var ");
181 |           addList(first, false, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
182 |         }
183 |         break;
184 | 
185 |       case Token.LABEL_NAME:
186 |         Preconditions.checkState(!n.getString().isEmpty());
187 |         addIdentifier(n.getString());
188 |         break;
189 | 
190 |       case Token.NAME:
191 |         if (first == null || first.isEmpty()) {
192 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
193 |         } else {
194 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
195 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
196 |           cc.addOp("=", true);
197 |           if (first.isComma()) {
198 |             addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.ASSIGN));
199 |           } else {
200 |             // Add expression, consider nearby code at lowest level of
201 |             // precedence.
202 |             addExpr(first, 0, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
203 |           }
204 |         }
205 |         break;
206 | 
207 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
208 |         add("[");
209 |         addArrayList(first);
210 |         add("]");
211 |         break;
212 | 
213 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST:
214 |         add("(");
215 |         addList(first);
216 |         add(")");
217 |         break;
218 | 
219 |       case Token.COMMA:
220 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
221 |         addList(first, false, context);
222 |         break;
223 | 
224 |       case Token.NUMBER:
225 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
226 |         cc.addNumber(n.getDouble());
227 |         break;
228 | 
229 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
230 |       case Token.VOID:
231 |       case Token.NOT:
232 |       case Token.BITNOT:
233 |       case Token.POS: {
234 |         // All of these unary operators are right-associative
235 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
236 |         cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
237 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type));
238 |         break;
239 |       }
240 | 
241 |       case Token.NEG: {
242 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
243 | 
244 |         // It's important to our sanity checker that the code
245 |         // we print produces the same AST as the code we parse back.
246 |         // NEG is a weird case because Rhino parses "- -2" as "2".
247 |         if (n.getFirstChild().isNumber()) {
248 |           cc.addNumber(-n.getFirstChild().getDouble());
249 |         } else {
250 |           cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
251 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type));
252 |         }
253 | 
254 |         break;
255 |       }
256 | 
257 |       case Token.HOOK: {
258 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
259 |         int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
260 |         addLeftExpr(first, p + 1, context);
261 |         cc.addOp("?", true);
262 |         addExpr(first.getNext(), 1);
263 |         cc.addOp(":", true);
264 |         addExpr(last, 1);
265 |         break;
266 |       }
267 | 
268 |       case Token.REGEXP:
269 |         if (!first.isString() ||
270 |             !last.isString()) {
271 |           throw new Error("Expected children to be strings");
272 |         }
273 | 
274 |         String regexp = regexpEscape(first.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder);
275 | 
276 |         // I only use one .add because whitespace matters
277 |         if (childCount == 2) {
278 |           add(regexp + last.getString());
279 |         } else {
280 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
281 |           add(regexp);
282 |         }
283 |         break;
284 | 
285 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
286 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
287 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
288 |         }
289 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
290 |         boolean funcNeedsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
291 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
292 |           add("(");
293 |         }
294 | 
295 |         add("function");
296 |         add(first);
297 | 
298 |         add(first.getNext());
299 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
300 |         cc.endFunction(context == Context.STATEMENT);
301 | 
302 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
303 |           add(")");
304 |         }
305 |         break;
306 | 
307 |       case Token.GETTER_DEF:
308 |       case Token.SETTER_DEF:
309 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getParent().isObjectLit());
310 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
311 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.isFunction());
312 | 
313 |         // Get methods are unnamed
314 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getFirstChild().getString().isEmpty());
315 |         if (type == Token.GETTER_DEF) {
316 |           // Get methods have no parameters.
317 |           Preconditions.checkState(!first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasChildren());
318 |           add("get ");
319 |         } else {
320 |           // Set methods have one parameter.
321 |           Preconditions.checkState(first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasOneChild());
322 |           add("set ");
323 |         }
324 | 
325 |         // The name is on the GET or SET node.
326 |         String name = n.getString();
327 |         Node fn = first;
328 |         Node parameters = fn.getChildAtIndex(1);
329 |         Node body = fn.getLastChild();
330 | 
331 |         // Add the property name.
332 |         if (!n.isQuotedString() &&
333 |             TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(name) &&
334 |             // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that were
335 |             // unicode escaped.
336 |             NodeUtil.isLatin(name)) {
337 |           add(name);
338 |         } else {
339 |           // Determine if the string is a simple number.
340 |           double d = getSimpleNumber(name);
341 |           if (!Double.isNaN(d)) {
342 |             cc.addNumber(d);
343 |           } else {
344 |             addJsString(n);
345 |           }
346 |         }
347 | 
348 |         add(parameters);
349 |         add(body, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
350 |         break;
351 | 
352 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
353 |       case Token.BLOCK: {
354 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
355 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
356 |         }
357 |         boolean preserveBlock = context == Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK;
358 |         if (preserveBlock) {
359 |           cc.beginBlock();
360 |         }
361 | 
362 |         boolean preferLineBreaks =
363 |             type == Token.SCRIPT ||
364 |             (type == Token.BLOCK &&
365 |                 !preserveBlock &&
366 |                 n.getParent() != null &&
367 |                 n.getParent().isScript());
368 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
369 |           add(c, Context.STATEMENT);
370 | 
371 |           // VAR doesn't include ';' since it gets used in expressions
372 |           if (c.isVar()) {
373 |             cc.endStatement();
374 |           }
375 | 
376 |           if (c.isFunction()) {
377 |             cc.maybeLineBreak();
378 |           }
379 | 
380 |           // Prefer to break lines in between top-level statements
381 |           // because top level statements are more homogeneous.
382 |           if (preferLineBreaks) {
383 |             cc.notePreferredLineBreak();
384 |           }
385 |         }
386 |         if (preserveBlock) {
387 |           cc.endBlock(cc.breakAfterBlockFor(n, context == Context.STATEMENT));
388 |         }
389 |         break;
390 |       }
391 | 
392 |       case Token.FOR:
393 |         if (childCount == 4) {
394 |           add("for(");
395 |           if (first.isVar()) {
396 |             add(first, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
397 |           } else {
398 |             addExpr(first, 0, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
399 |           }
400 |           add(";");
401 |           add(first.getNext());
402 |           add(";");
403 |           add(first.getNext().getNext());
404 |           add(")");
405 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
406 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
407 |         } else {
408 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
409 |           add("for(");
410 |           add(first);
411 |           add("in");
412 |           add(first.getNext());
413 |           add(")");
414 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
415 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
416 |         }
417 |         break;
418 | 
419 |       case Token.DO:
420 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
421 |         add("do");
422 |         addNonEmptyStatement(first, Context.OTHER, false);
423 |         add("while(");
424 |         add(last);
425 |         add(")");
426 |         cc.endStatement();
427 |         break;
428 | 
429 |       case Token.WHILE:
430 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
431 |         add("while(");
432 |         add(first);
433 |         add(")");
434 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
435 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
436 |         break;
437 | 
438 |       case Token.EMPTY:
439 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
440 |         break;
441 | 
442 |       case Token.GETPROP: {
443 |         Preconditions.checkState(
444 |             childCount == 2,
445 |             "Bad GETPROP: expected 2 children, but got %s", childCount);
446 |         Preconditions.checkState(
447 |             last.isString(),
448 |             "Bad GETPROP: RHS should be STRING");
449 |         boolean needsParens = (first.isNumber());
450 |         if (needsParens) {
451 |           add("(");
452 |         }
453 |         addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
454 |         if (needsParens) {
455 |           add(")");
456 |         }
457 |         add(".");
458 |         addIdentifier(last.getString());
459 |         break;
460 |       }
461 | 
462 |       case Token.GETELEM:
463 |         Preconditions.checkState(
464 |             childCount == 2,
465 |             "Bad GETELEM: expected 2 children but got %s", childCount);
466 |         addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
467 |         add("[");
468 |         add(first.getNext());
469 |         add("]");
470 |         break;
471 | 
472 |       case Token.WITH:
473 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
474 |         add("with(");
475 |         add(first);
476 |         add(")");
477 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
478 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
479 |         break;
480 | 
481 |       case Token.INC:
482 |       case Token.DEC: {
483 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
484 |         String o = type == Token.INC ? "++" : "--";
485 |         int postProp = n.getIntProp(Node.INCRDECR_PROP);
486 |         // A non-zero post-prop value indicates a post inc/dec, default of zero
487 |         // is a pre-inc/dec.
488 |         if (postProp != 0) {
489 |           addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
490 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
491 |         } else {
492 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
493 |           add(first);
494 |         }
495 |         break;
496 |       }
497 | 
498 |       case Token.CALL:
499 |         // We have two special cases here:
500 |         // 1) If the left hand side of the call is a direct reference to eval,
501 |         // then it must have a DIRECT_EVAL annotation. If it does not, then
502 |         // that means it was originally an indirect call to eval, and that
503 |         // indirectness must be preserved.
504 |         // 2) If the left hand side of the call is a property reference,
505 |         // then the call must not a FREE_CALL annotation. If it does, then
506 |         // that means it was originally an call without an explicit this and
507 |         // that must be preserved.
508 |         if (isIndirectEval(first)
509 |             || n.getBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL) && NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
510 |           add("(0,");
511 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.COMMA));
512 |           add(")");
513 |         } else {
514 |           addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
515 |         }
516 |         add("(");
517 |         addList(first.getNext());
518 |         add(")");
519 |         break;
520 | 
521 |       case Token.IF:
522 |         boolean hasElse = childCount == 3;
523 |         boolean ambiguousElseClause =
524 |             context == Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE && !hasElse;
525 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
526 |           cc.beginBlock();
527 |         }
528 | 
529 |         add("if(");
530 |         add(first);
531 |         add(")");
532 | 
533 |         if (hasElse) {
534 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
535 |               first.getNext(), Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE, false);
536 |           add("else");
537 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
538 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
539 |         } else {
540 |           addNonEmptyStatement(first.getNext(), Context.OTHER, false);
541 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
542 |         }
543 | 
544 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
545 |           cc.endBlock();
546 |         }
547 |         break;
548 | 
549 |       case Token.NULL:
550 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
551 |         add("null");
552 |         break;
553 | 
554 |       case Token.THIS:
555 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
556 |         add("this");
557 |         break;
558 | 
559 |       case Token.FALSE:
560 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
561 |         add("false");
562 |         break;
563 | 
564 |       case Token.TRUE:
565 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
566 |         add("true");
567 |         break;
568 | 
569 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
570 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
571 |         add("continue");
572 |         if (childCount == 1) {
573 |           if (!first.isLabelName()) {
574 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
575 |           }
576 |           add(" ");
577 |           add(first);
578 |         }
579 |         cc.endStatement();
580 |         break;
581 | 
582 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
583 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
584 |         add("debugger");
585 |         cc.endStatement();
586 |         break;
587 | 
588 |       case Token.BREAK:
589 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
590 |         add("break");
591 |         if (childCount == 1) {
592 |           if (!first.isLabelName()) {
593 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
594 |           }
595 |           add(" ");
596 |           add(first);
597 |         }
598 |         cc.endStatement();
599 |         break;
600 | 
601 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
602 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
603 |         add(first, Context.START_OF_EXPR);
604 |         cc.endStatement();
605 |         break;
606 | 
607 |       case Token.NEW:
608 |         add("new ");
609 |         int precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
610 | 
611 |         // If the first child contains a CALL, then claim higher precedence
612 |         // to force parentheses. Otherwise, when parsed, NEW will bind to the
613 |         // first viable parentheses (don't traverse into functions).
614 |         if (NodeUtil.containsType(
615 |             first, Token.CALL, NodeUtil.MATCH_NOT_FUNCTION)) {
616 |           precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(first.getType()) + 1;
617 |         }
618 |         addExpr(first, precedence);
619 | 
620 |         // '()' is optional when no arguments are present
621 |         Node next = first.getNext();
622 |         if (next != null) {
623 |           add("(");
624 |           addList(next);
625 |           add(")");
626 |         }
627 |         break;
628 | 
629 |       case Token.STRING:
630 |         if (childCount !=
631 |             ((n.getParent() != null &&
632 |               n.getParent().isObjectLit()) ? 1 : 0)) {
633 |           throw new IllegalStateException(
634 |               "Unexpected String children: " + n.getParent().toStringTree());
635 |         }
636 |         addJsString(n);
637 |         break;
638 | 
639 |       case Token.DELPROP:
640 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
641 |         add("delete ");
642 |         add(first);
643 |         break;
644 | 
645 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT: {
646 |         boolean needsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
647 |         if (needsParens) {
648 |           add("(");
649 |         }
650 |         add("{");
651 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
652 |           if (c != first) {
653 |             cc.listSeparator();
654 |           }
655 | 
656 |           if (c.isGetterDef() || c.isSetterDef()) {
657 |             add(c);
658 |           } else {
659 |             Preconditions.checkState(c.isString());
660 |             String key = c.getString();
661 |             // Object literal property names don't have to be quoted if they
662 |             // are not JavaScript keywords
663 |             if (!c.isQuotedString() &&
664 |                 !TokenStream.isKeyword(key) &&
665 |                 TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(key) &&
666 |                 // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that
667 |                 // were unicode escaped.
668 |                 NodeUtil.isLatin(key)) {
669 |               add(key);
670 |             } else {
671 |               // Determine if the string is a simple number.
672 |               double d = getSimpleNumber(key);
673 |               if (!Double.isNaN(d)) {
674 |                 cc.addNumber(d);
675 |               } else {
676 |                 addExpr(c, 1);
677 |               }
678 |             }
679 |             add(":");
680 |             addExpr(c.getFirstChild(), 1);
681 |           }
682 |         }
683 |         add("}");
684 |         if (needsParens) {
685 |           add(")");
686 |         }
687 |         break;
688 |       }
689 | 
690 |       case Token.SWITCH:
691 |         add("switch(");
692 |         add(first);
693 |         add(")");
694 |         cc.beginBlock();
695 |         addAllSiblings(first.getNext());
696 |         cc.endBlock(context == Context.STATEMENT);
697 |         break;
698 | 
699 |       case Token.CASE:
700 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
701 |         add("case ");
702 |         add(first);
703 |         addCaseBody(last);
704 |         break;
705 | 
706 |       case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
707 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
708 |         add("default");
709 |         addCaseBody(first);
710 |         break;
711 | 
712 |       case Token.LABEL:
713 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
714 |         if (!first.isLabelName()) {
715 |           throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
716 |         }
717 |         add(first);
718 |         add(":");
719 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
720 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), true);
721 |         break;
722 | 
723 |       default:
724 |         throw new Error("Unknown type " + type + "\n" + n.toStringTree());
725 |     }
726 | 
727 |     cc.endSourceMapping(n);
728 |   }

===== M0213 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1418-1437
Documentation:
/**
   * Compare this node to node2 recursively and return the first pair of nodes
   * that differs doing a preorder depth-first traversal. Package private for
   * testing. Returns null if the nodes are equivalent.
   */
Method code:
1418 |   NodeMismatch checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node node2) {
1419 |     if (!isEquivalentTo(node2, false, false)) {
1420 |       return new NodeMismatch(this, node2);
1421 |     }
1422 | 
1423 |     NodeMismatch res = null;
1424 |     Node n, n2;
1425 |     for (n = first, n2 = node2.first;
1426 |          res == null && n != null;
1427 |          n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1428 |       if (node2 == null) {
1429 |         throw new IllegalStateException();
1430 |       }
1431 |       res = n.checkTreeEqualsImpl(n2);
1432 |       if (res != null) {
1433 |         return res;
1434 |       }
1435 |     }
1436 |     return res;
1437 |   }

===== M0227 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.parseInputs()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1153-1291
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the externs and main inputs.
   *
   * @return A synthetic root node whose two children are the externs root
   *     and the main root
   */
Method code:
1153 |   Node parseInputs() {
1154 |     boolean devMode = options.devMode != DevMode.OFF;
1155 | 
1156 |     // If old roots exist (we are parsing a second time), detach each of the
1157 |     // individual file parse trees.
1158 |     if (externsRoot != null) {
1159 |       externsRoot.detachChildren();
1160 |     }
1161 |     if (jsRoot != null) {
1162 |       jsRoot.detachChildren();
1163 |     }
1164 | 
1165 |     // Parse main js sources.
1166 |     jsRoot = IR.block();
1167 |     jsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1168 | 
1169 |     externsRoot = IR.block();
1170 |     externsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1171 | 
1172 |     externAndJsRoot = IR.block(externsRoot, jsRoot);
1173 |     externAndJsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1174 | 
1175 |     if (options.tracer.isOn()) {
1176 |       tracker = new PerformanceTracker(jsRoot,
1177 |           options.tracer == TracerMode.ALL);
1178 |       addChangeHandler(tracker.getCodeChangeHandler());
1179 |     }
1180 | 
1181 |     Tracer tracer = newTracer("parseInputs");
1182 | 
1183 |     try {
1184 |       // Parse externs sources.
1185 |       for (CompilerInput input : externs) {
1186 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1187 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1188 |           return null;
1189 |         }
1190 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1191 |       }
1192 | 
1193 |       // Check if the sources need to be re-ordered.
1194 |       if (options.manageClosureDependencies) {
1195 |         for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1196 |           input.setCompiler(this);
1197 | 
1198 |           // Forward-declare all the provided types, so that they
1199 |           // are not flagged even if they are dropped from the process.
1200 |           for (String provide : input.getProvides()) {
1201 |             getTypeRegistry().forwardDeclareType(provide);
1202 |           }
1203 |         }
1204 | 
1205 |         try {
1206 |           inputs =
1207 |               (moduleGraph == null ? new JSModuleGraph(modules) : moduleGraph)
1208 |               .manageDependencies(
1209 |                   options.manageClosureDependenciesEntryPoints, inputs);
1210 |         } catch (CircularDependencyException e) {
1211 |           report(JSError.make(
1212 |               JSModule.CIRCULAR_DEPENDENCY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1213 |           return null;
1214 |         } catch (MissingProvideException e) {
1215 |           report(JSError.make(
1216 |               MISSING_ENTRY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1217 |           return null;
1218 |         }
1219 |       }
1220 | 
1221 |       // Check if inputs need to be rebuilt from modules.
1222 |       boolean staleInputs = false;
1223 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1224 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1225 | 
1226 |         // Inputs can have a null AST during initial parse.
1227 |         if (n == null) {
1228 |           continue;
1229 |         }
1230 | 
1231 |         if (n.getJSDocInfo() != null) {
1232 |           JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1233 |           if (info.isExterns()) {
1234 |             // If the input file is explicitly marked as an externs file, then
1235 |             // assume the programmer made a mistake and throw it into
1236 |             // the externs pile anyways.
1237 |             externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1238 |             input.setIsExtern(true);
1239 | 
1240 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1241 | 
1242 |             externs.add(input);
1243 |             staleInputs = true;
1244 |           } else if (info.isNoCompile()) {
1245 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1246 |             staleInputs = true;
1247 |           }
1248 |         }
1249 |       }
1250 | 
1251 |       if (staleInputs) {
1252 |         fillEmptyModules(modules);
1253 |         rebuildInputsFromModules();
1254 |       }
1255 | 
1256 |       // Build the AST.
1257 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1258 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1259 |         if (n == null) {
1260 |           continue;
1261 |         }
1262 | 
1263 |         if (devMode) {
1264 |           runSanityCheck();
1265 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1266 |             return null;
1267 |           }
1268 |         }
1269 | 
1270 |         if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null ||
1271 |             options.nameReferenceReportPath != null) {
1272 | 
1273 |           // Annotate the nodes in the tree with information from the
1274 |           // input file. This information is used to construct the SourceMap.
1275 |           SourceInformationAnnotator sia =
1276 |               new SourceInformationAnnotator(
1277 |                   input.getName(), options.devMode != DevMode.OFF);
1278 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(this, n, sia);
1279 |         }
1280 | 
1281 |         jsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1282 |       }
1283 | 
1284 |       if (hasErrors()) {
1285 |         return null;
1286 |       }
1287 |       return externAndJsRoot;
1288 |     } finally {
1289 |       stopTracer(tracer, "parseInputs");
1290 |     }
1291 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.isUnknownType()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 489-509
Documentation:
/**
   * We treat this as the unknown type if any of its implicit prototype
   * properties is unknown.
   */
Method code:
489 |   @Override
490 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
491 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
492 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.
493 |     if (unknown) {
494 |       ObjectType implicitProto = getImplicitPrototype();
495 |       if (implicitProto == null ||
496 |           implicitProto.isNativeObjectType()) {
497 |         unknown = false;
498 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
499 |           if (interfaceType.isUnknownType()) {
500 |             unknown = true;
501 |             break;
502 |           }
503 |         }
504 |       } else {
505 |         unknown = implicitProto.isUnknownType();
506 |       }
507 |     }
508 |     return unknown;
509 |   }

===== M0274 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GoogleCodingConvention.isConstant(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GoogleCodingConvention.java
Original range: 70-87
Documentation:
/**
   * {@inheritDoc}
   *
   * <p>This enforces the Google const name convention, that the first character
   * after the last $ must be an upper-case letter and all subsequent letters
   * must be upper case. The name must be at least 2 characters long.
   *
   * <p>Examples:
   * <pre>
   *      aaa          Not constant - lower-case letters in the name
   *      A            Not constant - too short
   *      goog$A       Constant - letters after the $ are upper-case.
   *      AA17         Constant - digits can appear after the first letter
   *      goog$7A      Not constant - first character after the $ must be
   *                   upper case.
   *      $A           Constant - doesn't have to be anything in front of the $
   * </pre>
   */
Method code:
70 |   @Override
71 |   public boolean isConstant(String name) {
72 |     if (name.length() <= 1) {
73 |       return false;
74 |     }
75 | 
76 |     // In compiled code, '$' is often a namespace delimiter. To allow inlining
77 |     // of namespaced constants, we strip off any namespaces here.
78 |     int pos = name.lastIndexOf('$');
79 |     if (pos >= 0) {
80 |       name = name.substring(pos + 1);
81 |       if (name.length() == 0) {
82 |         return false;
83 |       }
84 |     }
85 | 
86 |     return isConstantKey(name);
87 |   }

===== M0281 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.VerifyConstants.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 232-280
Documentation:

Method code:
232 |     @Override
233 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
234 |       if (n.isName()) {
235 |         String name = n.getString();
236 |         if (n.getString().isEmpty()) {
237 |           return;
238 |         }
239 | 
240 |         boolean isConst = n.getBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
241 |         if (checkUserDeclarations) {
242 |           boolean expectedConst = false;
243 |           CodingConvention convention = compiler.getCodingConvention();
244 |           if (NodeUtil.isConstantName(n)
245 |               || NodeUtil.isConstantByConvention(convention, n, parent)) {
246 |             expectedConst = true;
247 |           } else {
248 |             expectedConst = false;
249 | 
250 |             JSDocInfo info = null;
251 |             Var var = t.getScope().getVar(n.getString());
252 |             if (var != null) {
253 |               info = var.getJSDocInfo();
254 |             }
255 | 
256 |             if (info != null && info.isConstant()) {
257 |               expectedConst = true;
258 |             } else {
259 |               expectedConst = false;
260 |             }
261 |           }
262 | 
263 |           if (expectedConst) {
264 |             Preconditions.checkState(expectedConst == isConst,
265 |                 "The name %s is not annotated as constant.", name);
266 |           } else {
267 |             Preconditions.checkState(expectedConst == isConst,
268 |                 "The name %s should not be annotated as constant.", name);
269 |           }
270 |         }
271 | 
272 |         Boolean value = constantMap.get(name);
273 |         if (value == null) {
274 |           constantMap.put(name, isConst);
275 |         } else {
276 |           Preconditions.checkState(value.booleanValue() == isConst,
277 |               "The name %s is not consistently annotated as constant.", name);
278 |         }
279 |       }
280 |     }

===== M0287 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ClosureCodingConvention.getClassesDefinedByCall(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ClosureCodingConvention.java
Original range: 74-129
Documentation:
/**
   * {@inheritDoc}
   *
   * <p>Understands several different inheritance patterns that occur in
   * Google code (various uses of {@code inherits} and {@code mixin}).
   */
Method code:
74 |   @Override
75 |   public SubclassRelationship getClassesDefinedByCall(Node callNode) {
76 |     Node callName = callNode.getFirstChild();
77 |     SubclassType type = typeofClassDefiningName(callName);
78 |     if (type != null) {
79 |       Node subclass = null;
80 |       Node superclass = callNode.getLastChild();
81 | 
82 |       // There are six possible syntaxes for a class-defining method:
83 |       // SubClass.inherits(SuperClass)
84 |       // goog.inherits(SubClass, SuperClass)
85 |       // goog$inherits(SubClass, SuperClass)
86 |       // SubClass.mixin(SuperClass.prototype)
87 |       // goog.mixin(SubClass.prototype, SuperClass.prototype)
88 |       // goog$mixin(SubClass.prototype, SuperClass.prototype)
89 |       boolean isDeprecatedCall = callNode.getChildCount() == 2 &&
90 |           callName.isGetProp();
91 |       if (isDeprecatedCall) {
92 |         // SubClass.inherits(SuperClass)
93 |         subclass = callName.getFirstChild();
94 |       } else if (callNode.getChildCount() == 3) {
95 |         // goog.inherits(SubClass, SuperClass)
96 |         subclass = callName.getNext();
97 |       } else {
98 |         return null;
99 |       }
100 | 
101 |       if (type == SubclassType.MIXIN) {
102 |         // Only consider mixins that mix two prototypes as related to
103 |         // inheritance.
104 |         if (!endsWithPrototype(superclass)) {
105 |           return null;
106 |         }
107 |         if (!isDeprecatedCall) {
108 |           if (!endsWithPrototype(subclass)) {
109 |             return null;
110 |           }
111 |           // Strip off the prototype from the name.
112 |           subclass = subclass.getFirstChild();
113 |         }
114 |         superclass = superclass.getFirstChild();
115 |       }
116 | 
117 |       // bail out if either of the side of the "inherits"
118 |       // isn't a real class name. This prevents us from
119 |       // doing something weird in cases like:
120 |       // goog.inherits(MySubClass, cond ? SuperClass1 : BaseClass2)
121 |       if (subclass != null &&
122 |           subclass.isUnscopedQualifiedName() &&
123 |           superclass.isUnscopedQualifiedName()) {
124 |         return new SubclassRelationship(type, subclass, superclass);
125 |       }
126 |     }
127 | 
128 |     return null;
129 |   }

807 |   private void validateMaximumChildCount(Node n, int i) {

818 |     }
819 |   }
