===== M0301 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.RemoveUnusedVars.traverseNode(Node,Node,Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/RemoveUnusedVars.java
Original range: 192-296
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses everything in the current scope and marks variables that
   * are referenced.
   *
   * During traversal, we identify subtrees that will only be
   * referenced if their enclosing variables are referenced. Instead of
   * traversing those subtrees, we create a continuation for them,
   * and traverse them lazily.
   */
Method code:
192 |   private void traverseNode(Node n, Node parent, Scope scope) {
193 |     int type = n.getType();
194 |     Var var = null;
195 |     switch (type) {
196 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
197 |         // If this function is a removable var, then create a continuation
198 |         // for it instead of traversing immediately.
199 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(n)) {
200 |           var = scope.getVar(n.getFirstChild().getString());
201 |         }
202 | 
203 |         if (var != null && isRemovableVar(var)) {
204 |           continuations.put(var, new Continuation(n, scope));
205 |         } else {
206 |           traverseFunction(n, scope);
207 |         }
208 |         return;
209 | 
210 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
211 |         Assign maybeAssign = Assign.maybeCreateAssign(n);
212 |         if (maybeAssign != null) {
213 |           // Put this in the assign map. It might count as a reference,
214 |           // but we won't know that until we have an index of all assigns.
215 |           var = scope.getVar(maybeAssign.nameNode.getString());
216 |           if (var != null) {
217 |             assignsByVar.put(var, maybeAssign);
218 |             assignsByNode.put(maybeAssign.nameNode, maybeAssign);
219 | 
220 |             if (isRemovableVar(var) &&
221 |                 !maybeAssign.mayHaveSecondarySideEffects) {
222 |               // If the var is unreferenced and performing this assign has
223 |               // no secondary side effects, then we can create a continuation
224 |               // for it instead of traversing immediately.
225 |               continuations.put(var, new Continuation(n, scope));
226 |               return;
227 |             }
228 |           }
229 |         }
230 |         break;
231 | 
232 |       case Token.CALL:
233 |         // Look for calls to inheritance-defining calls (such as goog.inherits).
234 |         SubclassRelationship subclassRelationship =
235 |             codingConvention.getClassesDefinedByCall(n);
236 |         if (subclassRelationship != null) {
237 |           Var subclassVar = scope.getVar(subclassRelationship.subclassName);
238 |           // Don't try to track the inheritance calls for non-globals. It would
239 |           // be more correct to only not track when the subclass does not
240 |           // reference a constructor, but checking that it is a global is
241 |           // easier and mostly the same.
242 |           if (subclassVar != null && subclassVar.isGlobal()
243 |               && !referenced.contains(subclassVar)) {
244 |             // Save a reference to the EXPR node.
245 |             inheritsCalls.put(subclassVar, parent);
246 |             continuations.put(subclassVar, new Continuation(n, scope));
247 |             return;
248 |           }
249 |         }
250 |         break;
251 | 
252 |       case Token.NAME:
253 |         var = scope.getVar(n.getString());
254 |         if (parent.isVar()) {
255 |           Node value = n.getFirstChild();
256 |           if (value != null && var != null && isRemovableVar(var) &&
257 |               !NodeUtil.mayHaveSideEffects(value)) {
258 |             // If the var is unreferenced and creating its value has no side
259 |             // effects, then we can create a continuation for it instead
260 |             // of traversing immediately.
261 |             continuations.put(var, new Continuation(n, scope));
262 |             return;
263 |           }
264 |         } else {
265 | 
266 |           // If arguments is escaped, we just assume the worst and continue
267 |           // on all the parameters.
268 |           if ("arguments".equals(n.getString()) && scope.isLocal()) {
269 |             Node lp = scope.getRootNode().getFirstChild().getNext();
270 |             for (Node a = lp.getFirstChild(); a != null; a = a.getNext()) {
271 |               markReferencedVar(scope.getVar(a.getString()));
272 |             }
273 |           }
274 | 
275 |           // All name references that aren't declarations or assigns
276 |           // are references to other vars.
277 |           if (var != null) {
278 |             // If that var hasn't already been marked referenced, then
279 |             // start tracking it.  If this is an assign, do nothing
280 |             // for now.
281 |             if (isRemovableVar(var)) {
282 |               if (!assignsByNode.containsKey(n)) {
283 |                 markReferencedVar(var);
284 |               }
285 |             } else {
286 |               markReferencedVar(var);
287 |             }
288 |           }
289 |         }
290 |         break;
291 |     }
292 | 
293 |     for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
294 |       traverseNode(c, n, scope);
295 |     }
296 |   }

   * Converts a tree to js code

Method code:
609 |   private static String toSource(Node root, Format outputFormat,
610 |                                  boolean lineBreak,  int lineLengthThreshold,

/**
   * Converts an operator's token value (see {@link Token}) to a string
   * representation.
   *
   * @param operator the operator's token value to convert
   * @return the string representation or {@code null} if the token value is
   * not an operator
   */
Method code:
2127 |   static String opToStr(int operator) {
2128 |     switch (operator) {
2129 |       case Token.BITOR: return "|";
2130 |       case Token.OR: return "||";
2131 |       case Token.BITXOR: return "^";
2132 |       case Token.AND: return "&&";

2135 |       case Token.EQ: return "==";
2136 |       case Token.NOT: return "!";
2137 |       case Token.NE: return "!=";
2138 |       case Token.SHNE: return "!==";
2139 |       case Token.LSH: return "<<";
2140 |       case Token.IN: return "in";
2141 |       case Token.LE: return "<=";
2142 |       case Token.LT: return "<";
2143 |       case Token.URSH: return ">>>";
2144 |       case Token.RSH: return ">>";
2145 |       case Token.GE: return ">=";
2146 |       case Token.GT: return ">";
2147 |       case Token.MUL: return "*";
2148 |       case Token.DIV: return "/";
2149 |       case Token.MOD: return "%";
2150 |       case Token.BITNOT: return "~";
2151 |       case Token.ADD: return "+";
2152 |       case Token.SUB: return "-";
2153 |       case Token.POS: return "+";
2154 |       case Token.NEG: return "-";
2155 |       case Token.ASSIGN: return "=";
2156 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR: return "|=";
2157 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR: return "^=";
2158 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND: return "&=";
2159 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH: return "<<=";
2160 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH: return ">>=";
2161 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH: return ">>>=";
2162 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD: return "+=";

2164 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL: return "*=";

===== M0334 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.RemoveUnusedVars.interpretAssigns()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/RemoveUnusedVars.java
Original range: 704-749
Documentation:
/**
   * Look at all the property assigns to all variables.
   * These may or may not count as references. For example,
   *
   * <code>
   * var x = {};
   * x.foo = 3; // not a reference.
   * var y = foo();
   * y.foo = 3; // is a reference.
   * </code>
   *
   * Interpreting assignments could mark a variable as referenced that
   * wasn't referenced before, in order to keep it alive. Because we find
   * references by lazily traversing subtrees, marking a variable as
   * referenced could trigger new traversals of new subtrees, which could
   * find new references.
   *
   * Therefore, this interpretation needs to be run to a fixed point.
   */
Method code:
704 |   private void interpretAssigns() {
705 |     boolean changes = false;
706 |     do {
707 |       changes = false;
708 | 
709 |       // We can't use traditional iterators and iterables for this list,
710 |       // because our lazily-evaluated continuations will modify it while
711 |       // we traverse it.
712 |       for (int current = 0; current < maybeUnreferenced.size(); current++) {
713 |         Var var = maybeUnreferenced.get(current);
714 |         if (referenced.contains(var)) {
715 |           maybeUnreferenced.remove(current);
716 |           current--;
717 |         } else {
718 |           boolean assignedToUnknownValue = false;
719 |           boolean hasPropertyAssign = false;
720 | 
721 |           if (var.getParentNode().isVar() &&
722 |               !NodeUtil.isForIn(var.getParentNode().getParent())) {
723 |             Node value = var.getInitialValue();
724 |             assignedToUnknownValue = value != null &&
725 |                 !NodeUtil.isLiteralValue(value, true);
726 |           } else {
727 |             // This was initialized to a function arg or a catch param
728 |             // or a for...in variable.
729 |             assignedToUnknownValue = true;
730 |           }
731 | 
732 |           for (Assign assign : assignsByVar.get(var)) {
733 |             if (assign.isPropertyAssign) {
734 |               hasPropertyAssign = true;
735 |             } else if (!NodeUtil.isLiteralValue(
736 |                 assign.assignNode.getLastChild(), true)) {
737 |               assignedToUnknownValue = true;
738 |             }
739 |           }
740 | 
741 |           if (assignedToUnknownValue && hasPropertyAssign) {
742 |             changes = markReferencedVar(var) || changes;
743 |             maybeUnreferenced.remove(current);
744 |             current--;
745 |           }
746 |         }
747 |       }
748 |     } while (changes);
749 |   }

===== M0337 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.removeChild(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1752-1805
Documentation:
/** Safely remove children while maintaining a valid node structure. */
Method code:
1752 |   static void removeChild(Node parent, Node node) {
1753 |     if (isTryFinallyNode(parent, node)) {
1754 |       if (NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(getCatchBlock(parent))) {
1755 |         // A finally can only be removed if there is a catch.
1756 |         parent.removeChild(node);
1757 |       } else {
1758 |         // Otherwise only its children can be removed.
1759 |         node.detachChildren();
1760 |       }
1761 |     } else if (node.isCatch()) {
1762 |       // The CATCH can can only be removed if there is a finally clause.
1763 |       Node tryNode = node.getParent().getParent();
1764 |       Preconditions.checkState(NodeUtil.hasFinally(tryNode));
1765 |       node.detachFromParent();
1766 |     } else if (isTryCatchNodeContainer(node)) {
1767 |       // The container node itself can't be removed, but the contained CATCH
1768 |       // can if there is a 'finally' clause
1769 |       Node tryNode = node.getParent();
1770 |       Preconditions.checkState(NodeUtil.hasFinally(tryNode));
1771 |       node.detachChildren();
1772 |     } else if (node.isBlock()) {
1773 |       // Simply empty the block.  This maintains source location and
1774 |       // "synthetic"-ness.
1775 |       node.detachChildren();
1776 |     } else if (isStatementBlock(parent)
1777 |         || isSwitchCase(node)) {
1778 |       // A statement in a block can simply be removed.
1779 |       parent.removeChild(node);
1780 |     } else if (parent.isVar()) {
1781 |       if (parent.hasMoreThanOneChild()) {
1782 |         parent.removeChild(node);
1783 |       } else {
1784 |         // Remove the node from the parent, so it can be reused.
1785 |         parent.removeChild(node);
1786 |         // This would leave an empty VAR, remove the VAR itself.
1787 |         removeChild(parent.getParent(), parent);
1788 |       }
1789 |     } else if (parent.isLabel()
1790 |         && node == parent.getLastChild()) {
1791 |       // Remove the node from the parent, so it can be reused.
1792 |       parent.removeChild(node);
1793 |       // A LABEL without children can not be referred to, remove it.
1794 |       removeChild(parent.getParent(), parent);
1795 |     } else if (parent.isFor()
1796 |         && parent.getChildCount() == 4) {
1797 |       // Only Token.FOR can have an Token.EMPTY other control structure
1798 |       // need something for the condition. Others need to be replaced
1799 |       // or the structure removed.
1800 |       parent.replaceChild(node, IR.empty());
1801 |     } else {
1802 |       throw new IllegalStateException("Invalid attempt to remove node: " +
1803 |           node.toString() + " of "+ parent.toString());
1804 |     }
1805 |   }

===== M0340 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 331-358
Documentation:

Method code:
331 |   void setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType baseType, Node propertyNode) {
332 |     // This is a bit weird. We need to successfully handle these
333 |     // two cases:
334 |     // Foo.prototype = new Bar();
335 |     // and
336 |     // Foo.prototype = {baz: 3};
337 |     // In the first case, we do not want new properties to get
338 |     // added to Bar. In the second case, we do want new properties
339 |     // to get added to the type of the anonymous object.
340 |     //
341 |     // We handle this by breaking it into two cases:
342 |     //
343 |     // In the first case, we create a new PrototypeObjectType and set
344 |     // its implicit prototype to the type being assigned. This ensures
345 |     // that Bar will not get any properties of Foo.prototype, but properties
346 |     // later assigned to Bar will get inherited properly.
347 |     //
348 |     // In the second case, we just use the anonymous object as the prototype.
349 |     if (baseType.hasReferenceName() ||
350 |         isNativeObjectType() ||
351 |         baseType.isFunctionPrototypeType() ||
352 |         !(baseType instanceof PrototypeObjectType)) {
353 | 
354 |       baseType = new PrototypeObjectType(
355 |           registry, this.getReferenceName() + ".prototype", baseType);
356 |     }
357 |     setPrototype((PrototypeObjectType) baseType, propertyNode);
358 |   }

===== M0343 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.stop(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 407-434
Documentation:
/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silence_threshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
407 |   long stop(int silence_threshold) {
408 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
409 | 
410 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
411 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
412 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
413 |       return 0;
414 |     }
415 | 
416 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
417 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
418 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than
419 |       // extraTracingStatistics.size() because a new statistic may
420 |       // have been added
421 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
422 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
423 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
424 |       }
425 |     }
426 | 
427 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
428 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
429 |       return 0;
430 |     }
431 | 
432 |     trace.endEvent(this, silence_threshold);
433 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
434 |   }

===== M0355 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.moveNamedFunctions(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 575-607
Documentation:
/**
     * Move all the functions that are valid at the execution of the first
     * statement of the function to the beginning of the function definition.
     */
Method code:
575 |     private void moveNamedFunctions(Node functionBody) {
576 |       Preconditions.checkState(
577 |           functionBody.getParent().isFunction());
578 |       Node previous = null;
579 |       Node current = functionBody.getFirstChild();
580 |       // Skip any declarations at the beginning of the function body, they
581 |       // are already in the right place.
582 |       while (current != null && NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(current)) {
583 |         previous = current;
584 |         current = current.getNext();
585 |       }
586 | 
587 |       // Find any remaining declarations and move them.
588 |       Node insertAfter = previous;
589 |       while (current != null) {
590 |         // Save off the next node as the current node maybe removed.
591 |         Node next = current.getNext();
592 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(current)) {
593 |           // Remove the declaration from the body.
594 |           Preconditions.checkNotNull(previous);
595 |           functionBody.removeChildAfter(previous);
596 | 
597 |           // Readd the function at the top of the function body (after any
598 |           // previous declarations).
599 |           insertAfter = addToFront(functionBody, current, insertAfter);
600 |           reportCodeChange("Move function declaration not at top of function");
601 |         } else {
602 |           // Update the previous only if the current node hasn't been moved.
603 |           previous = current;
604 |         }
605 |         current = next;
606 |       }
607 |     }

===== M0394 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.Tracer(String,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 235-278
Documentation:
/**
   * Create and start a tracer.
   * Both type and comment may be null. See class comment for usage.
   *
   * @param type The type for totalling
   * @param comment Comment about this tracer
   */
Method code:
235 |   Tracer(@Nullable String type, @Nullable String comment) {
236 |     this.type = type;
237 |     this.comment = comment == null ? "" : comment;
238 |     startTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
239 |     startThread = Thread.currentThread();
240 |     if (!extraTracingStatistics.isEmpty()) {
241 |       int size = extraTracingStatistics.size();
242 |       extraTracingValues = new long[size];
243 |       int i = 0;
244 |       for (TracingStatistic tracingStatistic : extraTracingStatistics) {
245 |         extraTracingValues[i] = tracingStatistic.start(startThread);
246 |         i++;
247 |       }
248 |     }
249 | 
250 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
251 | 
252 |     // Do nothing if the current thread trace wasn't initialized.
253 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
254 |       return;
255 |     }
256 | 
257 |     // Check if we are creating too many Tracers.
258 |     if (trace.events.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
259 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
260 |                   "Giant thread trace. Too many Tracers created. "
261 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
262 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
263 |       trace.truncateEvents();
264 |     }
265 | 
266 |     // Check if we forgot to close the Tracers.
267 |     if (trace.outstandingEvents.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
268 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
269 |                   "Too many outstanding Tracers. Tracer.stop() is missing "
270 |                     + "or Tracer.stop() is not wrapped in a "
271 |                     + "try/finally block. "
272 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
273 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
274 |       trace.truncateOutstandingEvents();
275 |     }
276 | 
277 |     trace.startEvent(this);
278 |   }

===== M0402 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.TypeSafeDispatcher.process(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/TypeSafeDispatcher.java
Original range: 118-281
Documentation:

Method code:
118 |   public T process(AstNode node) {
119 |     switch (node.getType()) {
120 |       case Token.ADD:
121 |       case Token.AND:
122 |       case Token.BITAND:
123 |       case Token.BITOR:
124 |       case Token.BITXOR:
125 |       case Token.COMMA:
126 |       case Token.DIV:
127 |       case Token.EQ:
128 |       case Token.GE:
129 |       case Token.GT:
130 |       case Token.IN:
131 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
132 |       case Token.LE:
133 |       case Token.LSH:
134 |       case Token.LT:
135 |       case Token.MOD:
136 |       case Token.MUL:
137 |       case Token.NE:
138 |       case Token.OR:
139 |       case Token.RSH:
140 |       case Token.SHEQ:
141 |       case Token.SHNE:
142 |       case Token.SUB:
143 |       case Token.URSH:
144 |         return processInfixExpression((InfixExpression) node);
145 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
146 |         return processArrayLiteral((ArrayLiteral) node);
147 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
148 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
149 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
150 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
151 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
152 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
153 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
154 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
155 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
156 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
157 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
158 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
159 |         return processAssignment((Assignment) node);
160 |       case Token.BITNOT:
161 |       case Token.DEC:
162 |       case Token.DELPROP:
163 |       case Token.INC:
164 |       case Token.NEG:
165 |       case Token.NOT:
166 |       case Token.POS:
167 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
168 |       case Token.VOID:
169 |         return processUnaryExpression((UnaryExpression) node);
170 |       case Token.BLOCK:
171 |         if (node instanceof Block) {
172 |           return processBlock((Block) node);
173 |         } else  if (node instanceof Scope) {
174 |           return processScope((Scope) node);
175 |         } else {
176 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
177 |                                           node.getClass() +
178 |                                           " type: " +
179 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
180 |         }
181 |       case Token.BREAK:
182 |         return processBreakStatement((BreakStatement) node);
183 |       case Token.CALL:
184 |         return processFunctionCall((FunctionCall) node);
185 |       case Token.CASE:
186 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
187 |         return processSwitchCase((SwitchCase) node);
188 |       case Token.CATCH:
189 |         return processCatchClause((CatchClause) node);
190 |       case Token.COLON:
191 |         return processObjectProperty((ObjectProperty) node);
192 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
193 |         return processContinueStatement((ContinueStatement) node);
194 |       case Token.DO:
195 |         return processDoLoop((DoLoop) node);
196 |       case Token.EMPTY:
197 |         return processEmptyExpression((EmptyExpression) node);
198 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
199 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
200 |         if (node instanceof ExpressionStatement) {
201 |           return processExpressionStatement((ExpressionStatement) node);
202 |         } else  if (node instanceof LabeledStatement) {
203 |           return processLabeledStatement((LabeledStatement) node);
204 |         } else {
205 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
206 |                                           node.getClass() +
207 |                                           " type: " +
208 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
209 |         }
210 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
211 |       case Token.FALSE:
212 |       case Token.NULL:
213 |       case Token.THIS:
214 |       case Token.TRUE:
215 |         return processKeywordLiteral((KeywordLiteral) node);
216 |       case Token.FOR:
217 |         if (node instanceof ForInLoop) {
218 |           return processForInLoop((ForInLoop) node);
219 |         } else  if (node instanceof ForLoop) {
220 |           return processForLoop((ForLoop) node);
221 |         } else {
222 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
223 |                                           node.getClass() +
224 |                                           " type: " +
225 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
226 |         }
227 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
228 |         return processFunctionNode((FunctionNode) node);
229 |       case Token.GETELEM:
230 |         return processElementGet((ElementGet) node);
231 |       case Token.GETPROP:
232 |         return processPropertyGet((PropertyGet) node);
233 |       case Token.HOOK:
234 |         return processConditionalExpression((ConditionalExpression) node);
235 |       case Token.IF:
236 |         return processIfStatement((IfStatement) node);
237 |       case Token.LABEL:
238 |         return processLabel((Label) node);
239 |       case Token.LP:
240 |         return processParenthesizedExpression((ParenthesizedExpression) node);
241 |       case Token.NAME:
242 |         return processName((Name) node);
243 |       case Token.NEW:
244 |         return processNewExpression((NewExpression) node);
245 |       case Token.NUMBER:
246 |         return processNumberLiteral((NumberLiteral) node);
247 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
248 |         return processObjectLiteral((ObjectLiteral) node);
249 |       case Token.REGEXP:
250 |         return processRegExpLiteral((RegExpLiteral) node);
251 |       case Token.RETURN:
252 |         return processReturnStatement((ReturnStatement) node);
253 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
254 |         return processAstRoot((AstRoot) node);
255 |       case Token.STRING:
256 |         return processStringLiteral((StringLiteral) node);
257 |       case Token.SWITCH:
258 |         return processSwitchStatement((SwitchStatement) node);
259 |       case Token.THROW:
260 |         return processThrowStatement((ThrowStatement) node);
261 |       case Token.TRY:
262 |         return processTryStatement((TryStatement) node);
263 |       case Token.CONST:
264 |       case Token.VAR:
265 |         if (node instanceof VariableDeclaration) {
266 |           return processVariableDeclaration((VariableDeclaration) node);
267 |         } else  if (node instanceof VariableInitializer) {
268 |           return processVariableInitializer((VariableInitializer) node);
269 |         } else {
270 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
271 |                                           node.getClass() +
272 |                                           " type: " +
273 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
274 |         }
275 |       case Token.WHILE:
276 |         return processWhileLoop((WhileLoop) node);
277 |       case Token.WITH:
278 |         return processWithStatement((WithStatement) node);
279 |     }
280 |     return processIllegalToken(node);
281 |   }

76 |   public void enterScope(NodeTraversal t) {
77 |     Node declarationRoot = t.getScopeRoot();
78 |     Renamer renamer;
79 |     if (nameStack.isEmpty()) {
80 |       // If the contextual renamer is being used the starting context can not
81 |       // be a function.
82 |       Preconditions.checkState(
83 |           !declarationRoot.isFunction() ||
84 |           !(rootRenamer instanceof ContextualRenamer));

89 |     }
90 |

93 |       findDeclaredNames(declarationRoot, null, renamer);
94 |     }
95 |     nameStack.push(renamer);

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ClosureCodingConvention.typeofClassDefiningName(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ClosureCodingConvention.java
Original range: 136-157
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines whether the given node is a class-defining name, like
   * "inherits" or "mixin."
   * @return The type of class-defining name, or null.
   */
Method code:
136 |   private SubclassType typeofClassDefiningName(Node callName) {
137 |     // Check if the method name matches one of the class-defining methods.
138 |     String methodName = null;
139 |     if (callName.isGetProp()) {
140 |       methodName = callName.getLastChild().getString();
141 |     } else if (callName.isName()) {
142 |       String name = callName.getString();
143 |       int dollarIndex = name.lastIndexOf('$');
144 |       if (dollarIndex != -1) {
145 |         methodName = name.substring(dollarIndex + 1);
146 |       }
147 |     }
148 | 
149 |     if (methodName != null) {
150 |       if (methodName.equals("inherits")) {
151 |         return SubclassType.INHERITS;
152 |       } else if (methodName.equals("mixin")) {
153 |         return SubclassType.MIXIN;
154 |       }
155 |     }
156 |     return null;
157 |   }

===== M0437 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 989-991
Documentation:
/**
   * Checks whether {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that}.<p>
   *
   * Subtyping rules:
   * <ul>
   * <li>(unknown) &mdash; every type is a subtype of the Unknown type.</li>
   * <li>(no) &mdash; the No type is a subtype of every type.</li>
   * <li>(no-object) &mdash; the NoObject type is a subtype of every object
   * type (i.e. subtypes of the Object type).</li>
   * <li>(ref) &mdash; a type is a subtype of itself.</li>
   * <li>(union-l) &mdash; A union type is a subtype of a type U if all the
   * union type's constituents are a subtype of U. Formally<br>
   * {@code (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>) &lt;: U} if and only
   * {@code T<sub>k</sub> &lt;: U} for all {@code k &isin; 1..n}.</li>
   * <li>(union-r) &mdash; A type U is a subtype of a union type if it is a
   * subtype of one of the union type's constituents. Formally<br>
   * {@code U &lt;: (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>)} if and only
   * if {@code U &lt;: T<sub>k</sub>} for some index {@code k}.</li>
   * <li>(objects) &mdash; an Object {@code O<sub>1</sub>} is a subtype
   * of an object {@code O<sub>2</sub>} if it has more properties
   * than {@code O<sub>2</sub>} and all common properties are
   * pairwise subtypes.</li>
   * </ul>
   *
   * @return {@code this &lt;: that}
   */
Method code:
989 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
990 |     return isSubtypeHelper(this, that);
991 |   }

===== M0449 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.isImplicitPrototype(ObjectType)

Original range: 469-478
Documentation:
/**
   * Checks that the prototype is an implicit prototype of this object. Since
   * each object has an implicit prototype, an implicit prototype's
   * implicit prototype is also this implicit prototype's.
   *
   * @param prototype any prototype based object
   *
   * @return {@code true} if {@code prototype} is {@code equal} to any
   *         object in this object's implicit prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
469 |   final boolean isImplicitPrototype(ObjectType prototype) {
470 |     for (ObjectType current = this;
471 |          current != null;
472 |          current = current.getImplicitPrototype()) {
473 |       if (current.isEquivalentTo(prototype)) {
474 |         return true;
475 |       }
476 |     }
477 |     return false;
478 |   }

===== M0453 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1906-1908
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an function expression? An function expression is one
   * that has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of function expressions:
   * <pre>
   * (function () {})
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> expressions:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A node
   * @return Whether n is an function used within an expression.
   */
Method code:
1906 |   static boolean isFunctionExpression(Node n) {
1907 |     return n.isFunction() && !isStatement(n);
1908 |   }

===== M0454 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.toString(StringBuilder,boolean,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 927-986
Documentation:

Method code:
927 |   private void toString(
928 |       StringBuilder sb,
929 |       boolean printSource,
930 |       boolean printAnnotations,
931 |       boolean printType) {
932 |     sb.append(Token.name(type));
933 |     if (this instanceof StringNode) {
934 |       sb.append(' ');
935 |       sb.append(getString());
936 |     } else if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
937 |       sb.append(' ');
938 |       // In the case of JsDoc trees, the first child is often not a string
939 |       // which causes exceptions to be thrown when calling toString or
940 |       // toStringTree.
941 |       if (first == null || first.getType() != Token.NAME) {
942 |         sb.append("<invalid>");
943 |       } else {
944 |         sb.append(first.getString());
945 |       }
946 |     } else if (type == Token.NUMBER) {
947 |       sb.append(' ');
948 |       sb.append(getDouble());
949 |     }
950 |     if (printSource) {
951 |       int lineno = getLineno();
952 |       if (lineno != -1) {
953 |         sb.append(' ');
954 |         sb.append(lineno);
955 |       }
956 |     }
957 | 
958 |     if (printAnnotations) {
959 |       int[] keys = getSortedPropTypes();
960 |       for (int i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
961 |         int type = keys[i];
962 |         PropListItem x = lookupProperty(type);
963 |         sb.append(" [");
964 |         sb.append(propToString(type));
965 |         sb.append(": ");
966 |         String value;
967 |         switch (type) {
968 |           default:
969 |             value = x.toString();
970 |             break;
971 |         }
972 |         sb.append(value);
973 |         sb.append(']');
974 |       }
975 |     }
976 | 
977 |     if (printType) {
978 |       if (jsType != null) {
979 |         String jsTypeString = jsType.toString();
980 |         if (jsTypeString != null) {
981 |           sb.append(" : ");
982 |           sb.append(jsTypeString);
983 |         }
984 |       }
985 |     }
986 |   }

===== M0471 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLatin(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2403-2413
Documentation:
/** Test if all characters in the string are in the Basic Latin (aka ASCII)
   * character set - that they have UTF-16 values equal to or below 0x7f.
   * This check can find which identifiers with Unicode characters need to be
   * escaped in order to allow resulting files to be processed by non-Unicode
   * aware UNIX tools and editors.
   * *
   * See http://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Latin_characters_in_Unicode
   * for more on Basic Latin.
   *
   * @param s The string to be checked for ASCII-goodness.
   *
   * @return True if all characters in the string are in Basic Latin set.
   */
Method code:
2403 |   static boolean isLatin(String s) {
2404 |     char LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN = 0x7f;
2405 |     int len = s.length();
2406 |     for (int index = 0; index < len; index++) {
2407 |       char c = s.charAt(index);
2408 |       if (c > LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN) {
2409 |         return false;
2410 |       }
2411 |     }
2412 |     return true;
2413 |   }

===== M0484 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 617-629
Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
617 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
618 |     UnionTypeBuilder typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
619 |     if (typeSet == null) {
620 |       typeSet = new UnionTypeBuilder(this, PROPERTY_CHECKING_UNION_SIZE);
621 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
622 |     }
623 | 
624 |     typeSet.addAlternate(type);
625 |     addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, type);
626 | 
627 |     // Clear cached values that depend on typesIndexedByProperty.
628 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
629 |   }

===== M0501 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 317-345
Documentation:

Method code:
317 |     @Override
318 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
319 |       switch (n.getType()) {
320 |         case Token.WHILE:
321 |           if (CONVERT_WHILE_TO_FOR) {
322 |             Node expr = n.getFirstChild();
323 |             n.setType(Token.FOR);
324 |             Node empty = IR.empty();
325 |             empty.copyInformationFrom(n);

327 |             n.addChildAfter(empty.cloneNode(), expr);
328 |             reportCodeChange("WHILE node");
329 |           }

331 |

333 |           normalizeFunctionDeclaration(n);
334 |           break;

339 |         case Token.SETTER_DEF:

===== M0503 =====

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java

Documentation:
/** Gets the current scope. */
Method code:
595 |   public Scope getScope() {
596 |     Scope scope = scopes.isEmpty() ? null : scopes.peek();
597 |     if (scopeRoots.isEmpty()) {
598 |       return scope;
599 |     }
600 |

602 |     while (it.hasNext()) {

604 |       scopes.push(scope);
605 |     }
606 |     scopeRoots.clear();
607 | 
608 |     return scope;
609 |   }

===== M0506 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototype(PrototypeObjectType,Node)

/**
   * Sets the prototype.
   * @param prototype the prototype. If this value is {@code null} it will
   *        silently be discarded.
   */
Method code:
365 |   boolean setPrototype(PrototypeObjectType prototype, Node propertyNode) {
366 |     if (prototype == null) {
367 |       return false;
368 |     }
369 |     // getInstanceType fails if the function is not a constructor
370 |     if (isConstructor() && prototype == getInstanceType()) {
371 |       return false;
372 |     }
373 | 
374 |     PrototypeObjectType oldPrototype = prototypeSlot == null
375 |         ? null : (PrototypeObjectType) prototypeSlot.getType();
376 |     boolean replacedPrototype = oldPrototype != null;
377 | 
378 |     this.prototypeSlot = new Property("prototype", prototype, true,
379 |         propertyNode == null ? source : propertyNode);
380 |     prototype.setOwnerFunction(this);
381 | 
382 |     if (oldPrototype != null) {
383 |       // Disassociating the old prototype makes this easier to debug--
384 |       // we don't have to worry about two prototypes running around.
385 |       oldPrototype.setOwnerFunction(null);
386 |     }
387 | 
388 |     if (isConstructor() || isInterface()) {
389 |       FunctionType superClass = getSuperClassConstructor();
390 |       if (superClass != null) {
391 |         superClass.addSubType(this);
392 |       }
393 | 
394 |       if (isInterface()) {
395 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getExtendedInterfaces()) {
396 |           if (interfaceType.getConstructor() != null) {
397 |             interfaceType.getConstructor().addSubType(this);
398 |           }
399 |         }
400 |       }
401 |     }
402 | 
403 |     if (replacedPrototype) {
404 |       clearCachedValues();
405 |     }
406 | 
407 |     return true;
408 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.splitVarDeclarations(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 552-569
Documentation:
/**
     * Split a var node such as:
     *   var a, b;
     * into individual statements:
     *   var a;
     *   var b;
     * @param n The whose children we should inspect.
     */
Method code:
552 |     private void splitVarDeclarations(Node n) {
553 |       for (Node next, c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = next) {
554 |         next = c.getNext();
555 |         if (c.isVar()) {
556 |           if (assertOnChange && !c.hasChildren()) {
557 |             throw new IllegalStateException("Empty VAR node.");
558 |           }
559 | 
560 |           while (c.getFirstChild() != c.getLastChild()) {
561 |             Node name = c.getFirstChild();
562 |             c.removeChild(name);
563 |             Node newVar = IR.var(name).srcref(n);
564 |             n.addChildBefore(newVar, c);
565 |             reportCodeChange("VAR with multiple children");
566 |           }
567 |         }
568 |       }
569 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getBestJSDocInfo(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 3007-3028
Documentation:
/** Find the best JSDoc for the given node. */
Method code:
3007 |   static JSDocInfo getBestJSDocInfo(Node n) {
3008 |     JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
3009 |     if (info == null) {
3010 |       Node parent = n.getParent();
3011 |       if (parent == null) {
3012 |         return null;
3013 |       }
3014 | 
3015 |       if (parent.isName()) {
3016 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3017 |       } else if (parent.isAssign()) {
3018 |         info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
3019 |       } else if (isObjectLitKey(parent, parent.getParent())) {
3020 |         info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
3021 |       } else if (parent.isFunction()) {
3022 |         info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
3023 |       } else if (parent.isVar() && parent.hasOneChild()) {
3024 |         info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
3025 |       }
3026 |     }

===== M0533 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 510-545
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a function.
   */
Method code:
510 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
511 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
512 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.isFunction());
513 | 
514 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
515 | 
516 |     boolean isFunctionExpression = (parent != null)
517 |         && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n);
518 | 
519 |     if (!isFunctionExpression) {
520 |       // Functions declarations are in the scope containing the declaration.
521 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
522 |     }
523 | 
524 |     curNode = n;
525 |     pushScope(n);
526 | 
527 |     if (isFunctionExpression) {
528 |       // Function expression names are only accessible within the function
529 |       // scope.
530 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
531 |     }
532 | 
533 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
534 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
535 | 
536 |     // Args
537 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
538 | 
539 |     // Body
540 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null &&
541 |             body.isBlock());
542 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
543 | 
544 |     popScope();
545 |   }

===== M0536 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanVars(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 124-178
Documentation:
/**
   * Scans and gather variables declarations under a Node
   */
Method code:
124 |   private void scanVars(Node n, Node parent) {
125 |     switch (n.getType()) {
126 |       case Token.VAR:
127 |         // Declare all variables. e.g. var x = 1, y, z;
128 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
129 |              child != null;) {
130 |           Node next = child.getNext();
131 |           declareVar(child);
132 |           child = next;
133 |         }
134 |         return;
135 | 
136 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
137 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
138 |           return;
139 |         }
140 | 
141 |         String fnName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
142 |         if (fnName.isEmpty()) {
143 |           // This is invalid, but allow it so the checks can catch it.
144 |           return;
145 |         }
146 |         declareVar(n.getFirstChild());
147 |         return;   // should not examine function's children
148 | 
149 |       case Token.CATCH:
150 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 2);
151 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getFirstChild().isName());
152 |         // the first child is the catch var and the third child
153 |         // is the code block
154 | 
155 |         final Node var = n.getFirstChild();
156 |         final Node block = var.getNext();
157 | 
158 |         declareVar(var);
159 |         scanVars(block, n);
160 |         return;  // only one child to scan
161 | 
162 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
163 |         inputId = n.getInputId();
164 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(inputId);
165 |         break;
166 |     }
167 | 
168 |     // Variables can only occur in statement-level nodes, so
169 |     // we only need to traverse children in a couple special cases.
170 |     if (NodeUtil.isControlStructure(n) || NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(n)) {
171 |       for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
172 |            child != null;) {
173 |         Node next = child.getNext();
174 |         scanVars(child, n);
175 |         child = next;
176 |       }
177 |     }
178 |   }

===== M0548 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtypeHelper(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1014-1042
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
1014 |   static boolean isSubtypeHelper(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
1015 |     // unknown
1016 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
1017 |       return true;
1018 |     }
1019 |     // equality
1020 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
1021 |       return true;
1022 |     }
1023 |     // all type
1024 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
1025 |       return true;
1026 |     }
1027 |     // unions
1028 |     if (thatType.isUnionType()) {
1029 |       UnionType union = thatType.toMaybeUnionType();
1030 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
1031 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
1032 |           return true;
1033 |         }
1034 |       }
1035 |     }
1036 |     // proxy types
1037 |     if (thatType instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
1038 |       return thisType.isSubtype(
1039 |           ((ProxyObjectType) thatType).getReferencedTypeInternal());
1040 |     }
1041 |     return false;
1042 |   }

===== M0556 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addOp(String,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 203-231
Documentation:

Method code:
203 |   void addOp(String op, boolean binOp) {
204 |     maybeEndStatement();
205 | 
206 |     char first = op.charAt(0);
207 |     char prev = getLastChar();
208 | 
209 |     if ((first == '+' || first == '-') && prev == first) {
210 |       // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of
211 |       // things like "x + ++y" or "x++ + ++y"
212 |       append(" ");
213 |     } else if (Character.isLetter(first) &&
214 |                isWordChar(prev)) {
215 |       // Make sure there is a space after e.g. instanceof , typeof
216 |       append(" ");
217 |     } else if (prev == '-' && first == '>') {
218 |       // Make sure that we don't emit -->
219 |       append(" ");
220 |     }
221 | 
222 |     // Allow formating around the operator.
223 |     appendOp(op, binOp);
224 | 
225 |     // Line breaking after an operator is always safe. Line breaking before an
226 |     // operator on the other hand is not. We only line break after a bin op
227 |     // because it looks strange.
228 |     if (binOp) {
229 |       maybeCutLine();
230 |     }
231 |   }

===== M0563 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.java
Original range: 105-156
Documentation:

Method code:
105 |   @Override
106 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
107 | 
108 |     switch (n.getType()) {
109 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
110 |         {
111 |           // Add recursive function name, if needed.
112 |           // NOTE: "enterScope" is called after we need to pick up this name.
113 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
114 | 
115 |           // If needed, add the function recursive name.
116 |           String name = n.getFirstChild().getString();
117 |           if (name != null && !name.isEmpty() && parent != null
118 |               && !NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(n)) {
119 |             renamer.addDeclaredName(name);
120 |           }
121 | 
122 |           nameStack.push(renamer);
123 |         }
124 |         break;
125 | 
126 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST: {
127 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
128 | 
129 |           // Add the function parameters
130 |           for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
131 |             String name = c.getString();
132 |             renamer.addDeclaredName(name);
133 |           }
134 | 
135 |           // Add the function body declarations
136 |           Node functionBody = n.getNext();
137 |           findDeclaredNames(functionBody, null, renamer);
138 | 
139 |           nameStack.push(renamer);
140 |         }
141 |         break;
142 | 
143 |       case Token.CATCH:
144 |         {
145 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
146 | 
147 |           String name = n.getFirstChild().getString();
148 |           renamer.addDeclaredName(name);
149 | 
150 |           nameStack.push(renamer);
151 |         }
152 |         break;
153 |     }
154 | 
155 |     return true;
156 |   }

===== M0568 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 121-212
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
121 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
122 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
123 |     // just bail out early here.
124 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
125 |       return this;
126 |     }
127 | 
128 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
129 | 
130 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
131 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
132 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
133 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
134 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
135 |     }
136 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
137 |       if (alternate.isUnionType()) {
138 |         UnionType union = alternate.toMaybeUnionType();
139 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
140 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
141 |         }
142 |       } else {
143 |         if (alternates.size() > maxUnionSize) {
144 |           return this;
145 |         }
146 | 
147 |         // Function types are special, because they have their
148 |         // own bizarro sub-lattice. See the commants on
149 |         // FunctionType#supAndInf helper and above at functionTypePosition.
150 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType() && functionTypePosition != -1) {
151 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
152 |           FunctionType other =
153 |               alternates.get(functionTypePosition).toMaybeFunctionType();
154 |           FunctionType supremum =
155 |               alternate.toMaybeFunctionType().supAndInfHelper(other, true);
156 |           alternates.set(functionTypePosition, supremum);
157 |           result = null;
158 |           return this;
159 |         }
160 | 
161 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
162 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
163 |         // one another.
164 |         int currentIndex = 0;
165 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
166 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
167 |           JSType current = it.next();
168 | 
169 |           // Unknown and NoResolved types may just be names that haven't
170 |           // been resolved yet. So keep these in the union, and just use
171 |           // equality checking for simple de-duping.
172 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
173 |               current.isUnknownType() ||
174 |               alternate.isNoResolvedType() ||
175 |               current.isNoResolvedType()) {
176 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
177 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
178 |               return this;
179 |             }
180 |           } else {
181 |             if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
182 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
183 |               return this;
184 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
185 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
186 |               it.remove();
187 | 
188 |               if (currentIndex == functionTypePosition) {
189 |                 functionTypePosition = -1;
190 |               } else if (currentIndex < functionTypePosition) {
191 |                 functionTypePosition--;
192 |                 currentIndex--;
193 |               }
194 |             }
195 |           }
196 |           currentIndex++;
197 |         }
198 | 
199 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType()) {
200 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
201 |           Preconditions.checkState(functionTypePosition == -1);
202 |           functionTypePosition = alternates.size();
203 |         }
204 | 
205 |         alternates.add(alternate);
206 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
207 |       }
208 |     } else {
209 |       result = null;
210 |     }
211 |     return this;
212 |   }

/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.
   *
   * @param lastVarArgs whether the last type should considered as a variable
   *     length argument.
   * @param parameterTypes the parameter types. The last element of this array
   *     is considered a variable length argument is {@code lastVarArgs} is
   *     {@code true}.
   * @return a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list
   */
Method code:
1216 |   private Node createParameters(boolean lastVarArgs, JSType... parameterTypes) {
1217 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1218 |     int max = parameterTypes.length - 1;
1219 |     for (int i = 0; i <= max; i++) {
1220 |       if (lastVarArgs && i == max) {
1221 |         builder.addVarArgs(parameterTypes[i]);
1222 |       } else {
1223 |         builder.addRequiredParams(parameterTypes[i]);
1224 |       }
1225 |     }
1226 |     return builder.build();
1227 |   }

===== M0580 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.ParserRunner.parse(StaticSourceFile,String,Config,ErrorReporter,Logger)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/ParserRunner.java
Original range: 104-148
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
104 |   public static Node parse(StaticSourceFile sourceFile,
105 |                            String sourceString,
106 |                            Config config,
107 |                            ErrorReporter errorReporter,
108 |                            Logger logger) throws IOException {
109 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
110 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
111 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
112 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
113 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
114 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
115 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
116 |     // ES5 specifically allows trailing commas
117 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(
118 |         config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3);
119 | 
120 |     if (config.isIdeMode || config.languageMode != LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3) {
121 |       // Do our own identifier check for ECMASCRIPT 5
122 |       compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(true);
123 |       compilerEnv.setAllowKeywordAsObjectPropertyName(true);
124 |     }
125 | 
126 |     if (config.isIdeMode) {
127 |       compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(true);
128 |     }
129 |     compilerEnv.setIdeMode(config.isIdeMode);
130 | 
131 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
132 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
133 |     try {
134 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceFile.getName(), 1);
135 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
136 |       logger.info(
137 |           "Error parsing " + sourceFile.getName() + ": " + e.getMessage());
138 |     } finally {
139 |       Context.exit();
140 |     }
141 |     Node root = null;
142 |     if (astRoot != null) {
143 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
144 |           astRoot, sourceFile, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
145 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
146 |     }
147 |     return root;
148 |   }

===== M0584 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.process(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 119-150
Documentation:

Method code:
119 |   @Override
120 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
121 |     new NodeTraversal(
122 |         compiler, new NormalizeStatements(compiler, assertOnChange))
123 |         .traverseRoots(externs, root);
124 |     if (MAKE_LOCAL_NAMES_UNIQUE) {
125 |       MakeDeclaredNamesUnique renamer = new MakeDeclaredNamesUnique();
126 |       NodeTraversal t = new NodeTraversal(compiler, renamer);
127 |       t.traverseRoots(externs, root);
128 |     }
129 |     // It is important that removeDuplicateDeclarations runs after
130 |     // MakeDeclaredNamesUnique in order for catch block exception names to be
131 |     // handled properly. Specifically, catch block exception names are
132 |     // only valid within the catch block, but our currect Scope logic
133 |     // has no concept of this and includes it in the containing function
134 |     // (or global scope). MakeDeclaredNamesUnique makes the catch exception
135 |     // names unique so that removeDuplicateDeclarations() will properly handle
136 |     // cases where a function scope variable conflict with a exception name:
137 |     //   function f() {
138 |     //      try {throw 0;} catch(e) {e; /* catch scope 'e'*/}
139 |     //      var e = 1; // f scope 'e'
140 |     //   }
141 |     // otherwise 'var e = 1' would be rewritten as 'e = 1'.
142 |     // TODO(johnlenz): Introduce a seperate scope for catch nodes.
143 |     removeDuplicateDeclarations(externs, root);
144 |     new PropagateConstantAnnotationsOverVars(compiler, assertOnChange)
145 |         .process(externs, root);
146 | 
147 |     if (!compiler.getLifeCycleStage().isNormalized()) {
148 |       compiler.setLifeCycleStage(LifeCycleStage.NORMALIZED);
149 |     }
150 |   }

===== M0594 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.identifierEscape(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 1085-1105
Documentation:

Method code:
1085 |   static String identifierEscape(String s) {
1086 |     // First check if escaping is needed at all -- in most cases it isn't.
1087 |     if (NodeUtil.isLatin(s)) {
1088 |       return s;
1089 |     }
1090 | 
1091 |     // Now going through the string to escape non-latin characters if needed.
1092 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
1093 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
1094 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
1095 |       // Identifiers should always go to Latin1/ ASCII characters because
1096 |       // different browser's rules for valid identifier characters are
1097 |       // crazy.
1098 |       if (c > 0x1F && c < 0x7F) {
1099 |         sb.append(c);
1100 |       } else {
1101 |         appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1102 |       }
1103 |     }
1104 |     return sb.toString();
1105 |   }

===== M0598 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processFunctionNode(FunctionNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 600-658
Documentation:

Method code:
600 |     @Override
601 |     Node processFunctionNode(FunctionNode functionNode) {
602 |       Name name = functionNode.getFunctionName();
603 |       Boolean isUnnamedFunction = false;
604 |       if (name == null) {
605 |         int functionType = functionNode.getFunctionType();
606 |         if (functionType != FunctionNode.FUNCTION_EXPRESSION) {
607 |           errorReporter.error(
608 |             "unnamed function statement",
609 |             sourceName,
610 |             functionNode.getLineno(), "", 0);
611 |         }
612 |         name = new Name();
613 |         name.setIdentifier("");
614 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
615 |       }
616 |       Node node = newNode(Token.FUNCTION);
617 |       Node newName = transform(name);
618 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
619 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
620 |         // declaration.
621 |         newName.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
622 |         // TODO(bowdidge) Mark line number of paren correctly.
623 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
624 |         // same line as the function keyword.
625 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
626 |             functionNode.getLp();
627 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
628 |         maybeSetLengthFrom(newName, name);
629 |       }
630 | 
631 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
632 |       Node lp = newNode(Token.PARAM_LIST);
633 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
634 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
635 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
636 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
637 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
638 |       // TODO(bowdidge): Mark line number of paren correctly.
639 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
640 |       if (fnName != null) {
641 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
642 |       } else {
643 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
644 |       }
645 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
646 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
647 | 
648 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
649 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
650 |         lp.addChildToBack(transform(param));
651 |       }
652 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
653 | 
654 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
655 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
656 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);
657 |      return node;
658 |     }

===== M0599 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.precedence(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1161-1233
Documentation:
/*
   *  0 comma ,
   *  1 assignment = += -= *= /= %= <<= >>= >>>= &= ^= |=
   *  2 conditional ?:
   *  3 logical-or ||
   *  4 logical-and &&
   *  5 bitwise-or |
   *  6 bitwise-xor ^
   *  7 bitwise-and &
   *  8 equality == !=
   *  9 relational < <= > >=
   * 10 bitwise shift << >> >>>
   * 11 addition/subtraction + -
   * 12 multiply/divide * / %
   * 13 negation/increment ! ~ - ++ --
   * 14 call, member () [] .
   */
Method code:
1161 |   static int precedence(int type) {
1162 |     switch (type) {
1163 |       case Token.COMMA:  return 0;
1164 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
1165 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
1166 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
1167 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
1168 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
1169 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
1170 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
1171 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
1172 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
1173 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
1174 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
1175 |       case Token.ASSIGN: return 1;
1176 |       case Token.HOOK:   return 2;  // ?: operator
1177 |       case Token.OR:     return 3;
1178 |       case Token.AND:    return 4;
1179 |       case Token.BITOR:  return 5;
1180 |       case Token.BITXOR: return 6;
1181 |       case Token.BITAND: return 7;
1182 |       case Token.EQ:
1183 |       case Token.NE:
1184 |       case Token.SHEQ:
1185 |       case Token.SHNE:   return 8;
1186 |       case Token.LT:
1187 |       case Token.GT:
1188 |       case Token.LE:
1189 |       case Token.GE:
1190 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
1191 |       case Token.IN:     return 9;
1192 |       case Token.LSH:
1193 |       case Token.RSH:
1194 |       case Token.URSH:   return 10;
1195 |       case Token.SUB:
1196 |       case Token.ADD:    return 11;
1197 |       case Token.MUL:
1198 |       case Token.MOD:
1199 |       case Token.DIV:    return 12;
1200 |       case Token.INC:
1201 |       case Token.DEC:
1202 |       case Token.NEW:
1203 |       case Token.DELPROP:
1204 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
1205 |       case Token.VOID:
1206 |       case Token.NOT:
1207 |       case Token.BITNOT:
1208 |       case Token.POS:
1209 |       case Token.NEG:    return 13;
1210 | 
1211 |       case Token.CALL:
1212 |       case Token.GETELEM:
1213 |       case Token.GETPROP:
1214 |       // Data values
1215 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
1216 |       case Token.EMPTY:  // TODO(johnlenz): remove this.
1217 |       case Token.FALSE:
1218 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
1219 |       case Token.NAME:
1220 |       case Token.NULL:
1221 |       case Token.NUMBER:
1222 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
1223 |       case Token.REGEXP:
1224 |       case Token.STRING:
1225 |       case Token.THIS:
1226 |       case Token.TRUE:
1227 |         return 15;
1228 | 
1229 |       default: throw new Error("Unknown precedence for " +
1230 |                                Token.name(type) +
1231 |                                " (type " + type + ")");
1232 |     }
1233 |   }

Method code:
774 |   private void validateChildCount(Node n, int i) {
775 |     boolean valid = false;
776 |     if (i == 0) {
777 |       valid = !n.hasChildren();
778 |     } else if (i == 1) {
779 |       valid = n.hasOneChild();
780 |     } else {
781 |       valid = (n.getChildCount() == i);
782 |     }
783 |     if (!valid) {
784 |       violation(
785 |           "Expected " + i + " children, but was "
786 |               + n.getChildCount(), n);
787 |     }
788 |   }

===== M0616 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.RemoveUnusedVars.traverseFunction(Node,Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/RemoveUnusedVars.java
Original range: 325-339
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a function, which creates a new scope in javascript.
   *
   * Note that CATCH blocks also create a new scope, but only for the
   * catch variable. Declarations within the block actually belong to the
   * enclosing scope. Because we don't remove catch variables, there's
   * no need to treat CATCH blocks differently like we do functions.
   */
Method code:
325 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Scope parentScope) {
326 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
327 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.isFunction());
328 | 
329 |     final Node body = n.getLastChild();
330 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null &&
331 |             body.isBlock());
332 | 
333 |     Scope fnScope =
334 |         new SyntacticScopeCreator(compiler).createScope(n, parentScope);
335 |     traverseNode(body, n, fnScope);
336 | 
337 |     collectMaybeUnreferencedVars(fnScope);
338 |     allFunctionScopes.add(fnScope);
339 |   }

/**
   * Is this node a function declaration? A function declaration is a function
   * that has a name that is added to the current scope (i.e. a function that

155 |   public void validateExpression(Node n) {

204 |         return;

211 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:

220 |         return;

227 |       case Token.STRING:

===== M0635 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanRoot(Node,Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 84-119
Documentation:

Method code:
84 |   private void scanRoot(Node n, Scope parent) {
85 |     if (n.isFunction()) {
86 |       if (inputId == null) {
87 |         inputId = NodeUtil.getInputId(n);
88 |         // TODO(johnlenz): inputId maybe null if the FUNCTION node is detached
89 |         // from the AST.
90 |         // Is it meaningful to build a scope for detached FUNCTION node?
91 |       }
92 | 
93 |       final Node fnNameNode = n.getFirstChild();
94 |       final Node args = fnNameNode.getNext();
95 |       final Node body = args.getNext();
96 | 
97 |       // Bleed the function name into the scope, if it hasn't
98 |       // been declared in the outer scope.
99 |       String fnName = fnNameNode.getString();
100 |       if (!fnName.isEmpty() && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
101 |         declareVar(fnNameNode);
102 |       }
103 | 
104 |       // Args: Declare function variables
105 |       Preconditions.checkState(args.isParamList());
106 |       for (Node a = args.getFirstChild(); a != null;
107 |            a = a.getNext()) {
108 |         Preconditions.checkState(a.isName());
109 |         declareVar(a);
110 |       }
111 | 
112 |       // Body
113 |       scanVars(body, n);
114 |     } else {
115 |       // It's the global block
116 |       Preconditions.checkState(scope.getParent() == null);
117 |       scanVars(n, null);
118 |     }
119 |   }

/**
   * Add variable arguments to the end of the parameter list.
   * @return False if this is called after var args are added.
   */
Method code:
94 |   public boolean addVarArgs(JSType type) {
95 |     if (hasVarArgs()) {
96 |       return false;
97 |     }
98 | 
99 |     // There are two types of variable argument functions:
100 |     // 1) Programmer-defined var args
101 |     // 2) Native bottom types that can accept any argument.

103 |     // For the second, we do not want to cast it up to undefined.

106 |     }
107 |     newParameter(type).setVarArgs(true);

===== M0643 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 443-491
Documentation:

Method code:
443 |   @Override
444 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
445 |     if (JSType.isSubtypeHelper(this, that)) {
446 |       return true;
447 |     }
448 | 
449 |     // Union types
450 |     if (that.isUnionType()) {
451 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
452 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
453 |       return false;
454 |     }
455 | 
456 |     // record types
457 |     if (that.isRecordType()) {
458 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, that.toMaybeRecordType());
459 |     }
460 | 
461 |     // Interfaces
462 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
463 |     // to the interface instance.
464 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
465 |     ObjectType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
466 |     if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
467 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
468 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
469 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
470 |           return true;
471 |         }
472 |       }
473 |     }
474 | 
475 |     if (getConstructor() != null && getConstructor().isInterface()) {
476 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
477 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
478 |           return true;
479 |         }
480 |       }
481 |     }
482 | 
483 |     // other prototype based objects
484 |     if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
485 |       // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
486 |       // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
487 |       // to avoid guessing.
488 |       return true;
489 |     }
490 |     return this.isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
491 |   }

===== M0653 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 476-505
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
476 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
477 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
478 |     int type = n.getType();
479 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
480 |       inputId = n.getInputId();
481 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
482 |     }
483 | 
484 |     curNode = n;
485 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
486 | 
487 |     switch (type) {
488 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
489 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
490 |         break;
491 | 
492 |       default:
493 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
494 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
495 |           // would no longer point to the true next
496 |           Node next = child.getNext();
497 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
498 |           child = next;
499 |         }
500 |         break;
501 |     }
502 | 
503 |     curNode = n;
504 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
505 |   }

===== M0656 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.RemoveUnusedVars.isRemovableVar(Var)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/RemoveUnusedVars.java
Original range: 298-315
Documentation:

Method code:
298 |   private boolean isRemovableVar(Var var) {
299 |     // Global variables are off-limits if the user might be using them.
300 |     if (!removeGlobals && var.isGlobal()) {
301 |       return false;
302 |     }
303 | 
304 |     // Referenced variables are off-limits.
305 |     if (referenced.contains(var)) {
306 |       return false;
307 |     }
308 | 
309 |     // Exported variables are off-limits.
310 |     if (codingConvention.isExported(var.getName())) {
311 |       return false;
312 |     }
313 | 
314 |     return true;
315 |   }

459 |     @Override
460 |     void maybeCutLine() {
461 |       if (lineLength > lineLengthThreshold) {
462 |         // Use the preferred position provided it will break the line.

464 |             preferredBreakPosition < lineStartPosition + lineLength) {

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.RhinoErrorReporter.replacePlaceHolders(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/RhinoErrorReporter.java
Original range: 67-70
Documentation:
/**
   * For each message such as "Not a good use of {0}", replace the place
   * holder {0} with a wild card that matches all possible strings.
   * Also put the any non-place-holder in quotes for regex matching later.
   */
Method code:
67 |   private Pattern replacePlaceHolders(String s) {
68 |     s = Pattern.quote(s);
69 |     return Pattern.compile(s.replaceAll("\\{\\d+\\}", "\\\\E.*\\\\Q"));
70 |   }

   * Detach a child from its parent and siblings.
   */
Method code:
678 |   public void removeChild(Node child) {
679 |     Node prev = getChildBefore(child);
680 |     if (prev == null)
681 |         first = first.next;
682 |     else
683 |         prev.next = child.next;
684 |     if (child == last) last = prev;
685 |     child.next = null;
686 |     child.parent = null;
687 |   }

/**
   * Marks a var as referenced, recursing into any values of this var
   * that we skipped.
   * @return True if this variable had not been referenced before.
   */
Method code:
766 |   private boolean markReferencedVar(Var var) {
767 |     if (referenced.add(var)) {
768 |       for (Continuation c : continuations.get(var)) {
769 |         c.apply();
770 |       }
771 |       return true;
772 |     }
773 |     return false;
774 |   }

===== M0694 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PrepareAst.PrepareAnnotations.annotateFunctions(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PrepareAst.java
Original range: 223-245
Documentation:
/**
     * Annotate optional and var_arg function parameters.
     */
Method code:
223 |     private void annotateFunctions(Node n, Node parent) {
224 |       JSDocInfo fnInfo = NodeUtil.getFunctionJSDocInfo(n);
225 | 
226 |       // Compute which function parameters are optional and
227 |       // which are var_args.
228 |       Node args = n.getFirstChild().getNext();
229 |       for (Node arg = args.getFirstChild();
230 |            arg != null;
231 |            arg = arg.getNext()) {
232 |         String argName = arg.getString();
233 |         JSTypeExpression typeExpr = fnInfo == null ?
234 |             null : fnInfo.getParameterType(argName);
235 | 
236 |         if (convention.isOptionalParameter(arg) ||
237 |             typeExpr != null && typeExpr.isOptionalArg()) {
238 |           arg.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_OPTIONAL_PARAM, true);
239 |         }
240 |         if (convention.isVarArgsParameter(arg) ||
241 |             typeExpr != null && typeExpr.isVarArgs()) {
242 |           arg.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_VAR_ARGS_PARAM, true);
243 |         }
244 |       }
245 |     }
